
===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\docker-compose.yml =====

version: "3.9"

services:
  api:
    build:
      context: ./api
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    container_name: arcturus-api
    working_dir: /app
    env_file:
      - ./api/.env
    ports:
      - "4000:4000"
    command: npm run start:dev
    volumes:
      - ./api:/app
      - /app/node_modules

  workers:
    image: node:20-alpine
    container_name: arcturus-workers
    working_dir: /app
    env_file:
      - ./workers/.env
    command: sh -c "npm install && npm run start:dev -- scheduled"
    volumes:
      - ./workers:/app
      - /app/node_modules

  scrapers:
    image: node:20-alpine
    container_name: arcturus-scrapers
    working_dir: /app
    env_file:
      - ./scrapers/.env
    command: sh -c "npm install && npm run start:dev -- all"
    volumes:
      - ./scrapers:/app
      - /app/node_modules

	cd api && npm run prisma:seed

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\README.md =====

# Arcturus Backend
## Structure
- `api` вЂ” NestJS API
- `workers` вЂ” recompute jobs
- `scrapers` вЂ” market source ingestion
## Run order
1. Start api
2. Run scrapers
3. Run workers
4. Open app live mode
## API
### Setup
1. Create `.env` from `.env.example`
2. Install dependencies
3. Run prisma generate
4. Run migrations
5. Run seed
### Commands
- `npm run start:dev`
- `npm run prisma:generate`
- `npm run prisma:migrate:dev`
- `npm run prisma:seed`
## Workers
### Commands
- `npm run start:dev -- all`
- `npm run start:dev -- snapshots`
- `npm run start:dev -- decisions`
## Scrapers
### Commands
- `npm run start:dev -- all`
- `npm run start:dev -- olx`
- `npm run start:dev -- bricklink`
## Notes
Current scraper layer is parser-based and ready for iterative hardening.
Current market snapshots are normalized to UAH.

  api:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    container_name: arcturus-api
    restart: unless-stopped
    env_file:
      - .env
    ports:
      - "4000:4000"
    command: npm run start:dev
    volumes:
      - .:/app
      - /app/node_modules

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\Dockerfile =====

FROM node:20-alpine AS build
WORKDIR /app
COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm install
COPY . .
RUN npm run prisma:generate
RUN npm run build

FROM node:20-alpine
WORKDIR /app
COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm install --omit=dev
COPY --from=build /app/dist ./dist
COPY --from=build /app/prisma ./prisma
COPY --from=build /app/node_modules/.prisma ./node_modules/.prisma
EXPOSE 4000
CMD ["node", "dist/main"]

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\prisma\schema.prisma =====

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

model User {
  id                      String          @id @default(cuid())
  name                    String
  email                   String?
  role                    String          @default("operator")
  createdAt               DateTime        @default(now())
  updatedAt               DateTime        @updatedAt
  assignedInventoryItems  InventoryItem[] @relation("InventoryAssignedUser")
  assignedWatchlistItems  WatchlistItem[] @relation("WatchlistAssignedUser")
  notifications           Notification[]

  @@index([role])
  @@index([email])
}

model Activity {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  action    String
  meta      String?
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([action])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model Notification {
  id           String   @id @default(cuid())
  title        String
  message      String
  type         String   @default("info")
  read         Boolean  @default(false)
  targetUserId String?
  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())
  targetUser   User?    @relation(fields: [targetUserId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  @@index([targetUserId])
  @@index([type])
  @@index([read])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model Item {
  id               String           @id @default(cuid())
  kind             String           @default("set")
  setNumber        String?
  externalRef      String?
  title            String
  theme            String?
  subtheme         String?
  year             Int?
  conditionDefault String?
  createdAt        DateTime         @default(now())
  updatedAt        DateTime         @updatedAt
  inventoryItems   InventoryItem[]
  watchlistItems   WatchlistItem[]
  marketListings   MarketListing[]
  marketSnapshots  MarketSnapshot[]

  @@index([kind])
  @@index([setNumber])
  @@index([theme])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model InventoryItem {
  id                      String            @id @default(cuid())
  itemId                  String
  assignedUserId          String?
  titleSnapshot           String
  condition               String
  completenessPercent     Int
  sealed                  Boolean
  boxState                String?
  instructionsState       String?
  quantity                Int
  purchasePrice           Float
  extraCosts              Float
  totalCost               Float
  expectedSalePriceManual Float?
  notes                   String?
  acquiredAt              DateTime?
  createdAt               DateTime          @default(now())
  updatedAt               DateTime          @updatedAt
  item                    Item              @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  assignedUser            User?             @relation("InventoryAssignedUser", fields: [assignedUserId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  images                  InventoryImage[]
  sales                   Sale[]
  repriceFlowItems        RepriceFlowItem[]
  reviewFlowItems         ReviewFlowItem[]

  @@index([itemId])
  @@index([assignedUserId])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model InventoryImage {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  inventoryItemId String
  imageUrl        String
  storagePath     String
  sortOrder       Int           @default(0)
  isPrimary       Boolean       @default(false)
  createdAt       DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime      @updatedAt
  inventoryItem   InventoryItem @relation(fields: [inventoryItemId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([inventoryItemId])
  @@index([isPrimary])
  @@index([sortOrder])
}

model WatchlistItem {
  id                String             @id @default(cuid())
  itemId            String
  assignedUserId    String?
  titleSnapshot     String
  desiredBuyPrice   Float
  maxBuyPrice       Float
  targetSellPrice   Float?
  active            Boolean            @default(true)
  priority          Int                @default(50)
  notes             String?
  createdAt         DateTime           @default(now())
  updatedAt         DateTime           @updatedAt
  item              Item               @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  assignedUser      User?              @relation("WatchlistAssignedUser", fields: [assignedUserId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  purchaseFlowItems PurchaseFlowItem[]
  detectedDeals     DetectedDeal[]

  @@index([itemId])
  @@index([assignedUserId])
  @@index([active])
  @@index([priority])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model MarketSource {
  id             String          @id @default(cuid())
  code           String          @unique
  name           String
  type           String          @default("manual")
  enabled        Boolean         @default(true)
  createdAt      DateTime        @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime        @updatedAt
  marketListings MarketListing[]
  runLogs        SourceRunLog[]

  @@index([enabled])
  @@index([type])
}

model MarketListing {
  id                  String                 @id @default(cuid())
  sourceId            String
  itemId              String
  externalListingId   String
  titleRaw            String
  normalizedTitle     String?
  extractedSetNo      String?
  url                 String
  imageUrl            String?
  sellerName          String?
  sellerRating        Float?
  price               Float
  currency            String
  shippingPrice       Float?
  shippingCurrency    String?
  country             String?
  condition           String?
  sealed              Boolean?
  completenessPercent Int?
  quantityAvailable   Int?
  status              String
  fetchedAt           DateTime
  firstSeenAt         DateTime               @default(now())
  lastSeenAt          DateTime               @default(now())
  source              MarketSource           @relation(fields: [sourceId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  item                Item                   @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  unresolvedMatches   UnresolvedMatchQueue[]
  detectedDeals       DetectedDeal[]

  @@unique([sourceId, externalListingId])
  @@index([sourceId])
  @@index([itemId])
  @@index([externalListingId])
  @@index([extractedSetNo])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([fetchedAt])
}

model MarketSnapshot {
  id                      String   @id @default(cuid())
  itemId                  String
  scope                   String
  listingsCount           Int
  lowestPrice             Float?
  lowestPriceWithShipping Float?
  avgPrice                Float?
  medianPrice             Float?
  avgShipping             Float?
  minShipping             Float?
  maxShipping             Float?
  sealedAvgPrice          Float?
  usedAvgPrice            Float?
  confidenceScore         Float?
  computedAt              DateTime @default(now())
  item                    Item     @relation(fields: [itemId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([itemId])
  @@index([computedAt])
}

model DecisionSnapshot {
  id              String   @id @default(cuid())
  itemId          String
  contextType     String
  contextId       String
  action          String
  score           Float
  confidence      Float
  reasonPrimary   String
  reasonSecondary String?
  payloadJson     Json?
  createdAt       DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([itemId])
  @@index([contextType, contextId])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model PurchaseFlowItem {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  watchlistItemId String
  selectedPrice   Float
  selectedSource  String?
  status          String
  createdAt       DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime      @updatedAt
  watchlistItem   WatchlistItem @relation(fields: [watchlistItemId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([watchlistItemId])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model RepriceFlowItem {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  inventoryItemId String
  currentPrice    Float?
  suggestedPrice  Float?
  status          String
  createdAt       DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime      @updatedAt
  inventoryItem   InventoryItem @relation(fields: [inventoryItemId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([inventoryItemId])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model ReviewFlowItem {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  inventoryItemId String
  status          String
  reason          String?
  createdAt       DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime      @updatedAt
  inventoryItem   InventoryItem @relation(fields: [inventoryItemId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([inventoryItemId])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model DetectedDeal {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  watchlistItemId String
  listingId       String
  buyPrice        Float
  targetSellPrice Float?
  roiPercent      Float
  action          String
  createdAt       DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime      @updatedAt
  watchlistItem   WatchlistItem @relation(fields: [watchlistItemId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  listing         MarketListing @relation(fields: [listingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@unique([watchlistItemId, listingId])
  @@index([watchlistItemId])
  @@index([listingId])
  @@index([roiPercent])
  @@index([action])
}

model Sale {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  inventoryItemId String
  sellPrice       Float
  profit          Float
  createdAt       DateTime      @default(now())
  inventoryItem   InventoryItem @relation(fields: [inventoryItemId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([inventoryItemId])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model SoldComp {
  id              String    @id @default(cuid())
  sourceCode      String
  externalId      String
  title           String
  normalizedTitle String
  extractedSetNo  String?
  soldPrice       Float
  soldAt          DateTime?
  url             String?
  imageUrl        String?
  createdAt       DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime  @updatedAt

  @@unique([sourceCode, externalId])
  @@index([sourceCode])
  @@index([extractedSetNo])
  @@index([soldAt])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model ReserveRequest {
  id              String         @id @default(cuid())
  inventoryItemId String?
  productTitle    String
  name            String
  contact         String
  message         String?
  status          String         @default("pending")
  adminNote       String?
  createdAt       DateTime       @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime       @updatedAt

  @@index([inventoryItemId])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model ScanJob {
  id          String    @id @default(cuid())
  sourceCode  String
  query       String
  status      String    @default("queued")
  resultCount Int       @default(0)
  startedAt   DateTime?
  finishedAt  DateTime?
  createdAt   DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime  @updatedAt

  @@index([sourceCode])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model SourceRunLog {
  id           String       @id @default(cuid())
  sourceId     String
  startedAt    DateTime
  finishedAt   DateTime?
  status       String
  itemsSeen    Int          @default(0)
  itemsMatched Int          @default(0)
  itemsInserted Int         @default(0)
  itemsUpdated Int          @default(0)
  errorMessage String?
  createdAt    DateTime     @default(now())
  source       MarketSource @relation(fields: [sourceId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([sourceId])
  @@index([startedAt])
  @@index([status])
}

model SyncErrorLog {
  id          String   @id @default(cuid())
  scope       String
  referenceId String?
  sourceCode  String?
  message     String
  detailsJson Json?
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([scope])
  @@index([sourceCode])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model UnresolvedMatchQueue {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  listingId       String
  sourceCode      String
  titleRaw        String
  normalizedTitle String
  extractedSetNo  String?
  suggestedItemId String?
  status          String        @default("pending")
  operatorNote    String?
  createdAt       DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime      @updatedAt
  listing         MarketListing @relation(fields: [listingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([listingId])
  @@index([sourceCode])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\prisma\seed.ts =====

import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';

const prisma = new PrismaClient();

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  const admin = await prisma.user.upsert({
    where: {
      email: 'admin@arcturus.local',
    },
    update: {},
    create: {
      name: 'Admin',
      email: 'admin@arcturus.local',
      role: 'admin',
    },
  });

  const operator = await prisma.user.upsert({
    where: {
      email: 'operator@arcturus.local',
    },
    update: {},
    create: {
      name: 'Operator',
      email: 'operator@arcturus.local',
      role: 'operator',
    },
  });

  const sourceBrickLink = await prisma.marketSource.upsert({
    where: {
      code: 'bricklink',
    },
    update: {
      name: 'BrickLink',
      type: 'marketplace',
      enabled: true,
    },
    create: {
      code: 'bricklink',
      name: 'BrickLink',
      type: 'marketplace',
      enabled: true,
    },
  });

  const sourceOlx = await prisma.marketSource.upsert({
    where: {
      code: 'olx',
    },
    update: {
      name: 'OLX',
      type: 'marketplace',
      enabled: true,
    },
    create: {
      code: 'olx',
      name: 'OLX',
      type: 'marketplace',
      enabled: true,
    },
  });

  const placeholder = await prisma.item.upsert({
    where: {
      id: 'item_unresolved_placeholder',
    },
    update: {},
    create: {
      id: 'item_unresolved_placeholder',
      kind: 'unknown',
      title: 'UNRESOLVED_PLACEHOLDER',
      conditionDefault: 'unknown',
    },
  });

  const item70621 = await prisma.item.upsert({
    where: {
      id: 'item_70621',
    },
    update: {},
    create: {
      id: 'item_70621',
      kind: 'set',
      setNumber: '70621',
      title: 'Ninjago Vermillion Attack',
      theme: 'Ninjago',
      year: 2017,
      conditionDefault: 'used',
    },
  });

  const item70624 = await prisma.item.upsert({
    where: {
      id: 'item_70624',
    },
    update: {},
    create: {
      id: 'item_70624',
      kind: 'set',
      setNumber: '70624',
      title: 'Ninjago Vermillion Invader',
      theme: 'Ninjago',
      year: 2017,
      conditionDefault: 'used',
    },
  });

  const item75301 = await prisma.item.upsert({
    where: {
      id: 'item_sw_75301',
    },
    update: {},
    create: {
      id: 'item_sw_75301',
      kind: 'set',
      setNumber: '75301',
      title: 'Luke Skywalker X-Wing Fighter',
      theme: 'Star Wars',
      year: 2021,
      conditionDefault: 'used',
    },
  });

  await prisma.inventoryItem.upsert({
    where: {
      id: 'inv_70621',
    },
    update: {},
    create: {
      id: 'inv_70621',
      itemId: item70621.id,
      titleSnapshot: item70621.title,
      condition: 'used',
      completenessPercent: 100,
      sealed: false,
      boxState: 'no_box',
      instructionsState: 'pdf',
      quantity: 1,
      purchasePrice: 650,
      extraCosts: 50,
      totalCost: 700,
      expectedSalePriceManual: 1150,
      notes: 'Cleaned and complete',
      assignedUserId: operator.id,
    },
  });

  await prisma.inventoryItem.upsert({
    where: {
      id: 'inv_75301',
    },
    update: {},
    create: {
      id: 'inv_75301',
      itemId: item75301.id,
      titleSnapshot: item75301.title,
      condition: 'used',
      completenessPercent: 97,
      sealed: false,
      boxState: 'good',
      instructionsState: 'included',
      quantity: 1,
      purchasePrice: 1100,
      extraCosts: 80,
      totalCost: 1180,
      expectedSalePriceManual: 1699,
      notes: 'Small part check needed',

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\main.ts =====

import { ValidationPipe } from '@nestjs/common';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
import { AppModule } from './app.module';

async function bootstrap(): Promise<void> {
  const app = await NestFactory.create(AppModule);

  app.setGlobalPrefix('api');

  const allowedOrigins =
    process.env.ALLOWED_ORIGINS?.split(',').map((value) => value.trim()) ?? ['*'];

  app.enableCors({
    origin: allowedOrigins.includes('*') ? true : allowedOrigins,
    credentials: true,
  });

  app.useGlobalPipes(
    new ValidationPipe({
      whitelist: true,
      transform: true,
      forbidNonWhitelisted: true,
    }),
  );

  const port = process.env.PORT ? Number(process.env.PORT) : 4000;
  await app.listen(port);
}

void bootstrap();

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\common\activity.interceptor.ts =====

import {
  CallHandler,
  ExecutionContext,
  Injectable,
  NestInterceptor,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { Observable, tap } from 'rxjs';
import { ActivityService } from '../modules/activity/activity.service';

type RequestLike = {
  url?: string;
  method?: string;
  body?: unknown;
};

@Injectable()
export class ActivityInterceptor implements NestInterceptor {
  constructor(private readonly activity: ActivityService) {}

  intercept(context: ExecutionContext, next: CallHandler): Observable<unknown> {
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest<RequestLike>();

    return next.handle().pipe(
      tap({
        next: () => {
          const route = request.url ?? 'unknown_route';
          const method = request.method ?? 'UNKNOWN';

          void this.activity.log(`${method} ${route}`, {
            body: request.body ?? null,
          });
        },
      }),
    );
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\common\money.utils.ts =====

export function toMoney(value: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Number(value.toFixed(2));
}

export function ensurePositiveMoney(value: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value <= 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  return toMoney(value);
}

export function addMoney(...values: number[]): number {
  return toMoney(
    values.reduce((sum, value) => {
      if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
        return sum;
      }

      return sum + value;
    }, 0),
  );
}

export function subtractMoney(left: number, right: number): number {
  return toMoney(left - right);
}

export function calculateProfit(params: {
  revenue: number;
  cost: number;
}): number {
  return subtractMoney(params.revenue, params.cost);
}

export function calculateRoiPercent(params: {
  profit: number;
  cost: number;
}): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(params.cost) || params.cost <= 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Number(((params.profit / params.cost) * 100).toFixed(2));
}

export function calculateMarginPercent(params: {
  profit: number;
  revenue: number;
}): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(params.revenue) || params.revenue <= 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Number(((params.profit / params.revenue) * 100).toFixed(2));
}

export function clampMoney(value: number, min = 0, max = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
    return min;
  }

  return toMoney(Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value)));
}

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';

@Injectable()
export class ActivityService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  async log(action: string, meta?: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.prisma.activity.create({
      data: {
        action,
        meta: JSON.stringify(meta ?? {}),
      },
    });
  }

  async getAll(): Promise<unknown[]> {

      orderBy: {

    });
  }

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\ai\ai-suggestions.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  calculateRoiPercent,
  toMoney,
} from '../../common/money.utils';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { PricingService } from '../pricing/pricing.service';

@Injectable()
export class AiSuggestionsService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly pricing: PricingService,
  ) {}

  async getSuggestions(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const [watchlist, inventory] = await Promise.all([
      this.prisma.watchlistItem.findMany({
        take: 50,
        orderBy: {
          createdAt: 'desc',
        },
      }),
      this.prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
        take: 50,
        orderBy: {
          createdAt: 'desc',
        },
      }),
    ]);

    const buySuggestions = watchlist.map((item) => {
      const buyPrice = toMoney(item.maxBuyPrice ?? 0);
      const sellPrice = toMoney(item.targetSellPrice ?? 0);

      const roi = this.pricing.calculateROI({
        buyPrice,
        sellPrice,
      });

      return {
        type: 'buy',
        id: item.id,
        title: item.titleSnapshot,
        score: roi,
        suggestion:
          roi >= 60
            ? 'Aggressive buy candidate'
            : roi >= 35
              ? 'Good buy candidate'
              : 'Low priority buy',
      };
    });

    const sellSuggestions = inventory.map((item) => {
      const buyPrice = toMoney(item.totalCost ?? 0);
      const sellPrice = toMoney(item.expectedSalePriceManual ?? 0);

      const roi = this.pricing.calculateROI({
        buyPrice,
        sellPrice,
      });

      return {
        type: 'sell',
        id: item.id,
        title: item.titleSnapshot,
        score: roi,
        suggestion:
          roi >= 70
            ? 'Strong sell opportunity'
            : roi >= 40
              ? 'Ready to list'
              : 'Hold and monitor',
      };
    });

    return [...buySuggestions, ...sellSuggestions]
      .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)
      .slice(0, 25);
  }

  async explainDeal(body: {
    buyPrice: number;
    sellPrice: number;
    marketFloor?: number | null;
    marketAverage?: number | null;
    liquidityScore?: number | null;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    const buyPrice = toMoney(body.buyPrice);
    const sellPrice = toMoney(body.sellPrice);
    const profit = toMoney(sellPrice - buyPrice);

    const roi = calculateRoiPercent({
      profit,
      cost: buyPrice,
    });

    const liquidity = body.liquidityScore ?? 0.5;

    let verdict = 'Hold / weak deal';

    if (roi >= 60 && liquidity >= 0.6) {
      verdict = 'Strong deal';
    } else if (roi >= 35) {
      verdict = 'Usable deal';
    }

    const reasons = [
      `ROI is ${roi.toFixed(2)}%.`,
      body.marketFloor != null
        ? `Market floor is ${toMoney(body.marketFloor)}.`
        : 'No market floor provided.',
      body.marketAverage != null
        ? `Market average is ${toMoney(body.marketAverage)}.`
        : 'No market average provided.',
      `Liquidity score is ${liquidity.toFixed(2)}.`,
    ];

    return {
      buyPrice,
      sellPrice,
      profit,
      roi,
      verdict,
      reasons,
    };
  }
}

import {
  Body,
  Controller,
  Get,
  Post,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from '@nestjs/common';

@Controller('auth')
export class AuthController {
  @Post('login')
  login(
    @Body()
    body: {
      token: string;
    },
  ): {
    ok: boolean;
    token: string;
    user: {
      id: string;
      name: string;
      role: string;
    };
  } {
    const token = String(body.token ?? '').trim();

    if (!token || token !== process.env.ADMIN_TOKEN) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid token');
    }

    return {
      ok: true,
      token,
      user: {
        id: 'admin',
        name: 'Admin',
        role: 'admin',
      },
    };
  }

  @Get('me')
  me(): {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    role: string;
  } {
    return {
      id: 'admin',
      name: 'Admin',
      role: 'admin',
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\auth\auth.guard.ts =====

import {
  CanActivate,
  ExecutionContext,
  Injectable,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from '@nestjs/common';

type RequestWithHeaders = {
  headers: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
  user?: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    role: string;
  };
};

@Injectable()
export class AuthGuard implements CanActivate {
  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest<RequestWithHeaders>();
    const authHeader = request.headers.authorization;

    if (!authHeader || typeof authHeader !== 'string') {
      throw new UnauthorizedException('Missing token');
    }

    if (!authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid authorization header');
    }

    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();

    if (!token || token !== process.env.ADMIN_TOKEN) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid token');
    }

    request.user = {
      id: 'admin',
      name: 'Admin',
      role: 'admin',
    };

    return true;
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\auth\auth.service.ts =====

import { Injectable, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

export type AuthUser = {
  sub: string;
  email?: string | null;
  role: string;
  rawUser?: unknown;
};

@Injectable()
export class AuthService {
  async verifyToken(token: string): Promise<AuthUser> {
    const cleanToken = token.trim();

    if (!cleanToken) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException('Missing token');
    }

    if (process.env.ADMIN_TOKEN && cleanToken === process.env.ADMIN_TOKEN) {
      return {
        sub: 'admin',
        email: 'admin@arcturus.local',
        role: 'admin',
      };
    }

    const supabaseUrl = process.env.SUPABASE_URL;
    const serviceRoleKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;

    if (!supabaseUrl || !serviceRoleKey) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException('Supabase auth is not configured');
    }

    const client = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey);
    const result = await client.auth.getUser(cleanToken);

    if (result.error || !result.data.user) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid token');
    }

    return {
      sub: result.data.user.id,
      email: result.data.user.email,
      role:
        (result.data.user.app_metadata?.role as string | undefined) ??
        'operator',
      rawUser: result.data.user,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\auth\jwt-auth.guard.ts =====

import {
  CanActivate,
  ExecutionContext,
  Injectable,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { AuthService } from './auth.service';

type RequestWithHeaders = {
  headers: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
  user?: unknown;
};

@Injectable()
export class JwtAuthGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(private readonly authService: AuthService) {}

  async canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest<RequestWithHeaders>();
    const authHeader = request.headers.authorization;

    if (!authHeader || typeof authHeader !== 'string') {
      throw new UnauthorizedException('Missing authorization header');
    }

    if (!authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid authorization header');
    }

    const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '').trim();

    if (!token) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException('Missing token');
    }

    request.user = await this.authService.verifyToken(token);

    return true;
  }
}

import { SetMetadata } from '@nestjs/common';

export const ROLES_KEY = 'roles';

export function Roles(...roles: string[]): MethodDecorator & ClassDecorator {
  return SetMetadata(ROLES_KEY, roles);
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\auth\roles.guard.ts =====

import {
  CanActivate,
  ExecutionContext,
  ForbiddenException,
  Injectable,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import { ROLES_KEY } from './roles.decorator';

type RequestUser = {
  role?: string;
};

type RequestWithUser = {
  user?: RequestUser;
};

@Injectable()
export class RolesGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(private readonly reflector: Reflector) {}

  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    const requiredRoles =
      this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<string[]>(ROLES_KEY, [
        context.getHandler(),
        context.getClass(),
      ]) ?? [];

    if (requiredRoles.length === 0) {
      return true;
    }

    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest<RequestWithUser>();
    const user = request.user;

    if (!user?.role) {
      throw new ForbiddenException('No user role');
    }

    if (!requiredRoles.includes(user.role)) {
      throw new ForbiddenException('Forbidden');
    }

    return true;
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\collaboration\collaboration.controller.ts =====

import { Body, Controller, Get, Patch, Post } from '@nestjs/common';

  }

  @Patch('assign/inventory')

    return this.service.assignWatchlist(body);
  }

  @Get('assignments')
  getAssignments() {
    return this.service.getAssignments();
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\collaboration\collaboration.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { ActivityService } from '../activity/activity.service';
import { NotificationsService } from '../notifications/notifications.service';
import { RealtimeGateway } from '../realtime/realtime.gateway';

@Injectable()
export class CollaborationService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly activity: ActivityService,
    private readonly notifications: NotificationsService,
    private readonly realtime: RealtimeGateway,
  ) {}

  async getUsers() {
    return this.prisma.user.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'asc',
      },
    });
  }

  async createUser(body: {
    name: string;
    email?: string | null;
    role?: string | null;
  }) {
    const user = await this.prisma.user.create({
      data: {
        name: body.name,
        email: body.email ?? null,
        role: body.role ?? 'operator',
      },
    });

    await this.activity.log('user.created', {
      userId: user.id,
      name: user.name,
      role: user.role,
    });

    await this.notifications.create({
      title: 'User created',
      message: `${user.name} added as ${user.role}`,
      type: 'user',
    });

    this.realtime.emitEvent('user_created', user);

    return user;
  }

  async assignInventory(body: {
    inventoryItemId: string;
    userId: string;
  }) {
    const updated = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.update({
      where: {
        id: body.inventoryItemId,
      },
      data: {
        assignedUserId: body.userId,
      },
      include: {
        assignedUser: true,
        item: true,
      },
    });

    await this.activity.log('inventory.assigned', {
      inventoryItemId: body.inventoryItemId,
      userId: body.userId,
    });

    await this.notifications.create({
      title: 'Inventory assigned',
      message: `${updated.titleSnapshot} assigned to ${updated.assignedUser?.name ?? body.userId}`,
      type: 'assignment',
      targetUserId: body.userId,
    });

    this.realtime.emitInventoryUpdated(updated);

    return updated;
  }

  async assignWatchlist(body: {
    watchlistItemId: string;
    userId: string;
  }) {
    const updated = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.update({
      where: {
        id: body.watchlistItemId,
      },
      data: {
        assignedUserId: body.userId,
      },
      include: {
        assignedUser: true,
      },
    });

    await this.activity.log('watchlist.assigned', {
      watchlistItemId: body.watchlistItemId,
      userId: body.userId,
    });

    await this.notifications.create({
      title: 'Watchlist assigned',
      message: `${updated.titleSnapshot} assigned to ${updated.assignedUser?.name ?? body.userId}`,
      type: 'assignment',
      targetUserId: body.userId,
    });

    this.realtime.emitWatchlistUpdated(updated);

    return updated;
  }

  async getAssignments() {
    const [inventory, watchlist] = await Promise.all([
      this.prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
        where: {
          assignedUserId: {
            not: null,
          },
        },
        include: {
          assignedUser: true,
          item: true,
        },
        orderBy: {
          createdAt: 'desc',
        },
      }),
      this.prisma.watchlistItem.findMany({
        where: {
          assignedUserId: {
            not: null,
          },
        },
        include: {
          assignedUser: true,
        },
        orderBy: {
          createdAt: 'desc',
        },
      }),
    ]);

    return {
      inventory,
      watchlist,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\comps\comps.service.ts =====

import { BadRequestException, Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';

@Injectable()
export class CompsService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  normalizeTitle(title: string): string {
    return title
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s-]+/gu, ' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim();
  }

  extractSetNumber(title: string): string | null {
    const match = title.match(/\b\d{4,7}\b/);
    return match?.[0] ?? null;
  }

  async ingestSoldComps(body: {
    sourceCode: string;
    comps: Array<{
      externalId: string;
      title: string;
      soldPrice: number;
      soldAt?: string | null;
      url?: string | null;
      imageUrl?: string | null;
    }>;
  }): Promise<unknown[]> {
    if (!body.sourceCode?.trim()) {
      throw new BadRequestException('sourceCode is required');
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(body.comps)) {
      throw new BadRequestException('comps must be an array');
    }

    const rows = [];

    for (const comp of body.comps) {
      if (!comp.externalId?.trim() || !comp.title?.trim()) {
        continue;
      }

      if (!Number.isFinite(comp.soldPrice) || comp.soldPrice <= 0) {
        continue;
      }

      const normalizedTitle = this.normalizeTitle(comp.title);
      const extractedSetNo = this.extractSetNumber(comp.title);

      const upserted = await this.prisma.soldComp.upsert({
        where: {
          sourceCode_externalId: {
            sourceCode: body.sourceCode,
            externalId: comp.externalId,
          },
        },
        update: {
          title: comp.title,
          normalizedTitle,
          extractedSetNo,
          soldPrice: comp.soldPrice,
          soldAt: comp.soldAt ? new Date(comp.soldAt) : null,
          url: comp.url ?? null,
          imageUrl: comp.imageUrl ?? null,
        },
        create: {
          sourceCode: body.sourceCode,
          externalId: comp.externalId,
          title: comp.title,
          normalizedTitle,
          extractedSetNo,
          soldPrice: comp.soldPrice,
          soldAt: comp.soldAt ? new Date(comp.soldAt) : null,
          url: comp.url ?? null,
          imageUrl: comp.imageUrl ?? null,
        },
      });

      rows.push(upserted);
    }

    return rows;
  }

  async getSoldComps(params?: {
    sourceCode?: string;
    limit?: number;
  }): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.soldComp.findMany({
      where: params?.sourceCode
        ? {
            sourceCode: params.sourceCode,
          }
        : undefined,
      orderBy: [
        {
          soldAt: 'desc',
        },
        {
          createdAt: 'desc',
        },
      ],
      take: params?.limit ?? 100,
    });
  }

  async getCompSummary(body: {
    inventoryItemId?: string | null;
    itemId?: string | null;
    title?: string | null;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    let setNumber: string | null = null;
    let titleSeed = body.title ?? null;

    if (body.inventoryItemId) {
      const inventory = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
        where: {
          id: body.inventoryItemId,
        },
        include: {
          item: true,
        },
      });

      if (!inventory) {
        throw new NotFoundException('Inventory item not found');
      }

      setNumber = inventory.item?.setNumber ?? null;
      titleSeed = inventory.titleSnapshot ?? inventory.item?.title ?? null;
    }

    if (!setNumber && body.itemId) {
      const item = await this.prisma.item.findUnique({
        where: {
          id: body.itemId,
        },
      });

      if (!item) {
        throw new NotFoundException('Item not found');
      }

      setNumber = item.setNumber ?? null;
      titleSeed = titleSeed ?? item.title;
    }

    if (!setNumber && !titleSeed) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'inventoryItemId, itemId, or title is required',
      );
    }

    const comps = await this.prisma.soldComp.findMany({
      where: setNumber
        ? {
            extractedSetNo: setNumber,
          }
        : {
            normalizedTitle: {
              contains: this.normalizeTitle(titleSeed ?? '').slice(0, 18),
              mode: 'insensitive',
            },
          },
      orderBy: [
        {
          soldAt: 'desc',
        },
        {
          createdAt: 'desc',
        },
      ],
      take: 50,
    });

    if (comps.length === 0) {
      return {
        count: 0,
        min: null,
        avg: null,
        max: null,
        recent: [],
      };
    }

    const prices = comps
      .map((item) => Number(item.soldPrice))
      .filter((value) => Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0);

    if (prices.length === 0) {
      return {
        count: 0,
        min: null,
        avg: null,
        max: null,
        recent: [],
      };
    }

    const min = Math.min(...prices);
    const max = Math.max(...prices);
    const avg = prices.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / prices.length;

    return {
      count: comps.length,
      min,
      avg: Number(avg.toFixed(2)),
      max,
      recent: comps.slice(0, 10),
    };
  }
}

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InsightsService } from '../insights/insights.service';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';

@Injectable()
export class DashboardService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly insightsService: InsightsService,
  ) {}

  async getFlowCounters(): Promise<{
    purchaseFlow: number;
    repriceFlow: number;
    reviewFlow: number;
  }> {
    const [purchaseFlow, repriceFlow, reviewFlow] = await Promise.all([
      this.prisma.purchaseFlowItem.count({
        where: {
          status: {
            in: ['queued', 'pending'],
          },
        },
      }),
      this.prisma.repriceFlowItem.count({
        where: {
          status: {
            in: ['queued', 'pending'],
          },
        },
      }),
      this.prisma.reviewFlowItem.count({
        where: {
          status: {
            in: ['queued', 'pending'],
          },
        },
      }),
    ]);

    return {
      purchaseFlow,
      repriceFlow,
      reviewFlow,
    };
  }

  async getDashboardSummary(limit = 20): Promise<unknown> {
    const [
      flowCounters,
      inventoryCount,
      watchlistCount,
      activeListingsCount,
      soldCount,
      sourceCount,
      insights,
    ] = await Promise.all([
      this.getFlowCounters(),
      this.prisma.inventoryItem.count(),
      this.prisma.watchlistItem.count({
        where: {
          active: true,
        },
      }),
      this.prisma.marketListing.count({
        where: {
          status: 'active',
        },
      }),
      this.prisma.sale.count(),
      this.prisma.marketSource.count({
        where: {
          enabled: true,
        },
      }),
      this.insightsService.getDashboardInsights(limit),
    ]);

    return {
      counters: {
        inventory: inventoryCount,
        activeWatchlist: watchlistCount,
        activeListings: activeListingsCount,
        sales: soldCount,
        enabledSources: sourceCount,
        flows: flowCounters,
      },
      insights,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\deals\deals.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { NotificationsService } from '../notifications/notifications.service';
import { RealtimeGateway } from '../realtime/realtime.gateway';

@Injectable()
export class DealsService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly notifications: NotificationsService,
    private readonly realtime: RealtimeGateway,
  ) {}

  async detectDeals(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const [watchlist, listings] = await Promise.all([
      this.prisma.watchlistItem.findMany({
        where: { active: true },
        include: { item: true },
      }),
      this.prisma.marketListing.findMany({
        orderBy: { fetchedAt: 'desc' },
        take: 1000,
        include: {
          item: true,
          source: true,
        },
      }),
    ]);

    const results: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];

    for (const wish of watchlist) {
      for (const listing of listings) {
        const title = listing.titleRaw || listing.item?.title || wish.titleSnapshot;
        const normalizedListingTitle = title.toLowerCase();
        const normalizedWishTitle = wish.titleSnapshot.toLowerCase();

        const matchedByItem = wish.itemId === listing.itemId;
        const matchedBySet =
          wish.item?.setNumber != null &&
          title.toLowerCase().includes(wish.item.setNumber.toLowerCase());

        const matchedByTitle = normalizedListingTitle.includes(
          normalizedWishTitle.slice(0, 12),
        );

        if (!matchedByItem && !matchedBySet && !matchedByTitle) {
          continue;
        }

        const sell = Number(wish.targetSellPrice ?? 0);
        const buy = Number(listing.price ?? 0);
        const roiPercent = buy > 0 ? ((sell - buy) / buy) * 100 : 0;

        const action =
          roiPercent >= 60 ? 'BUY_NOW' : roiPercent >= 35 ? 'GOOD_DEAL' : 'SKIP';

        const existing = await this.prisma.detectedDeal.findFirst({
          where: {
            watchlistItemId: wish.id,
            listingId: listing.id,
          },
        });

        const persisted = existing
          ? await this.prisma.detectedDeal.update({
              where: { id: existing.id },
              data: {
                buyPrice: buy,
                targetSellPrice: sell || null,
                roiPercent,
                action,
              },
            })
          : await this.prisma.detectedDeal.create({
              data: {
                watchlistItemId: wish.id,
                listingId: listing.id,
                buyPrice: buy,
                targetSellPrice: sell || null,
                roiPercent,
                action,
              },
            });

        if (action === 'BUY_NOW') {
          await this.notifications.createDealNotification({
            itemTitle: title,
            roi: roiPercent,
            action,
          });

          this.realtime.emitNotification({
            title: 'Auto deal alert',
            message: `${title} вЂў ROI ${roiPercent.toFixed(2)}% вЂў ${action}`,
            type: 'deal',
          });
        }

        results.push({
          id: persisted.id,
          watchlistItemId: persisted.watchlistItemId,
          listingId: persisted.listingId,
          title,
          sourceCode: listing.source?.code ?? 'unknown',
          buyPrice: persisted.buyPrice,
          targetSellPrice: persisted.targetSellPrice,
          roiPercent: persisted.roiPercent,
          action: persisted.action,
        });
      }
    }

    return results.sort(
      (a, b) => Number(b.roiPercent ?? 0) - Number(a.roiPercent ?? 0),
    );
  }

  async getDeals(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.detectedDeal.findMany({
      include: {
        watchlistItem: true,
        listing: {
          include: {
            source: true,
            item: true,
          },
        },
      },
      orderBy: {
        roiPercent: 'desc',
      },
    });
  }
}

import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { RealtimeModule } from '../realtime/realtime.module';
import { DecisionsController } from './decisions.controller';
import { DecisionsService } from './decisions.service';

@Module({
  imports: [RealtimeModule],
  controllers: [DecisionsController],
  providers: [DecisionsService],
  exports: [DecisionsService],
})
export class DecisionsModule {}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\decisions\decisions.service.ts =====

import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { RealtimeGateway } from '../realtime/realtime.gateway';

@Injectable()
export class DecisionsService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly realtime: RealtimeGateway,
  ) {}

  async recomputeInventoryDecision(inventoryItemId: string): Promise<unknown> {
    const inventoryItem = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: inventoryItemId,
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
      },
    });

    if (!inventoryItem) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Inventory item not found');
    }

    const snapshot = await this.prisma.marketSnapshot.findFirst({
      where: {
        itemId: inventoryItem.itemId,
      },
      orderBy: {
        computedAt: 'desc',
      },
    });

    const marketMedian =
      snapshot?.medianPrice ?? inventoryItem.expectedSalePriceManual ?? 0;
    const marketLowest = snapshot?.lowestPriceWithShipping ?? marketMedian;
    const totalCost = inventoryItem.totalCost;
    const expectedManual = inventoryItem.expectedSalePriceManual ?? marketMedian;
    const margin = expectedManual - totalCost;

    let action = 'review';
    let score = 50;
    const confidence = snapshot?.confidenceScore ?? 0.4;
    let reasonPrimary = 'Needs manual review';
    let reasonSecondary = 'No strong rule fired';

    if (margin >= 300 && marketLowest >= totalCost * 1.3) {
      action = 'sell';
      score = 82;
      reasonPrimary = 'Healthy margin available';
      reasonSecondary = 'Market supports sale';
    } else if (marketMedian > expectedManual + 100) {
      action = 'reprice';
      score = 78;
      reasonPrimary = 'Price below market median';
      reasonSecondary = 'Repricing likely improves return';
    } else if (margin >= 120) {
      action = 'hold';
      score = 63;
      reasonPrimary = 'Position is acceptable';
      reasonSecondary = 'No urgent action needed';
    } else if (margin <= 50) {
      action = 'review';
      score = 88;
      reasonPrimary = 'Weak margin';
      reasonSecondary = 'Manual intervention recommended';
    }

    const decision = await this.prisma.decisionSnapshot.create({
      data: {
        itemId: inventoryItem.itemId,
        contextType: 'inventory',
        contextId: inventoryItem.id,
        action,
        score,
        confidence,
        reasonPrimary,
        reasonSecondary,
        payloadJson: {
          totalCost,
          marketMedian,
          marketLowest,
          expectedManual,
          margin,
        },
      },
    });

    this.realtime.emitItemRefresh(inventoryItem.itemId, 'inventory_decision_recomputed');
    this.realtime.emitDashboardRefresh('inventory_decision_recomputed');
    this.realtime.emitOpportunityRefresh('inventory');

    return decision;
  }

  async recomputeWatchlistDecision(watchlistItemId: string): Promise<unknown> {
    const watchlistItem = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: watchlistItemId,
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
      },
    });

    if (!watchlistItem) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Watchlist item not found');
    }

    const snapshot = await this.prisma.marketSnapshot.findFirst({
      where: {
        itemId: watchlistItem.itemId,
      },
      orderBy: {
        computedAt: 'desc',
      },
    });

    const lowestWithShipping =
      snapshot?.lowestPriceWithShipping ?? watchlistItem.maxBuyPrice;
    const targetSell = watchlistItem.targetSellPrice ?? 0;
    const spread = targetSell - lowestWithShipping;

    let action = 'wait';
    let score = 45;
    const confidence = snapshot?.confidenceScore ?? 0.4;
    let reasonPrimary = 'Wait for better entry';
    let reasonSecondary = 'Price is not strong enough';

    if (lowestWithShipping <= watchlistItem.maxBuyPrice && spread >= 250) {
      action = 'buy';
      score = 86;
      reasonPrimary = 'Inside max buy zone';
      reasonSecondary = 'Spread supports entry';
    } else if (lowestWithShipping <= watchlistItem.maxBuyPrice && spread >= 120) {
      action = 'buy';
      score = 72;
      reasonPrimary = 'Acceptable buy zone';
      reasonSecondary = 'Spread is workable';
    }

    const decision = await this.prisma.decisionSnapshot.create({
      data: {
        itemId: watchlistItem.itemId,
        contextType: 'watchlist',
        contextId: watchlistItem.id,
        action,
        score,
        confidence,
        reasonPrimary,
        reasonSecondary,
        payloadJson: {
          maxBuyPrice: watchlistItem.maxBuyPrice,
          desiredBuyPrice: watchlistItem.desiredBuyPrice,
          targetSell,
          lowestWithShipping,
          spread,
        },
      },
    });

    this.realtime.emitItemRefresh(watchlistItem.itemId, 'watchlist_decision_recomputed');
    this.realtime.emitDashboardRefresh('watchlist_decision_recomputed');
    this.realtime.emitOpportunityRefresh('watchlist');

    return decision;
  }

  async getLatestInventoryDecision(inventoryItemId: string): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.prisma.decisionSnapshot.findFirst({
      where: {
        contextType: 'inventory',
        contextId: inventoryItemId,
      },
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
    });
  }

  async getLatestWatchlistDecision(watchlistItemId: string): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.prisma.decisionSnapshot.findFirst({
      where: {
        contextType: 'watchlist',
        contextId: watchlistItemId,
      },
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
    });
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\flows\flows-events.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { RealtimeGateway } from '../realtime/realtime.gateway';

@Injectable()
export class FlowsEventsService {
  constructor(private readonly realtimeGateway: RealtimeGateway) {}

  purchaseChanged(): void {
    this.realtimeGateway.emitFlowRefresh('purchase');
    this.realtimeGateway.emitDashboardRefresh('purchase_flow_changed');
  }

  repriceChanged(): void {
    this.realtimeGateway.emitFlowRefresh('reprice');
    this.realtimeGateway.emitDashboardRefresh('reprice_flow_changed');
  }

  reviewChanged(): void {
    this.realtimeGateway.emitFlowRefresh('review');
    this.realtimeGateway.emitDashboardRefresh('review_flow_changed');
  }

  itemChanged(itemId: string): void {
    this.realtimeGateway.emitItemRefresh(itemId, 'flow_execution_changed');
    this.realtimeGateway.emitDashboardRefresh('item_changed');
  }

  opportunitiesChanged(): void {
    this.realtimeGateway.emitOpportunityRefresh('all');
    this.realtimeGateway.emitDashboardRefresh('opportunities_changed');
  }
}

import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { FlowsEventsService } from './flows-events.service';

@Injectable()
export class FlowsService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly events: FlowsEventsService,
  ) {}

  async getPurchaseFlow(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.purchaseFlowItem.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
      include: {
        watchlistItem: {
          include: {
            item: true,
            assignedUser: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });
  }

  async addToPurchaseFlow(watchlistItemId: string): Promise<unknown> {
    const item = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: watchlistItemId,
      },
    });

    if (!item) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Watchlist item not found');
    }

    const created = await this.prisma.purchaseFlowItem.create({
      data: {
        watchlistItemId,
        selectedPrice: item.desiredBuyPrice,
        selectedSource: 'manual',
        status: 'queued',
      },
      include: {
        watchlistItem: true,
      },
    });

    this.events.purchaseChanged();

    return created;
  }

  async updatePurchaseFlowStatus(
    id: string,
    status: string,
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    const updated = await this.prisma.purchaseFlowItem.update({
      where: {
        id,
      },
      data: {
        status,
      },
      include: {
        watchlistItem: true,
      },
    });

    this.events.purchaseChanged();

    return updated;
  }

  async getRepriceFlow(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.repriceFlowItem.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: {
          include: {
            item: true,
            assignedUser: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });
  }

  async addToRepriceFlow(inventoryItemId: string): Promise<unknown> {
    const item = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: inventoryItemId,
      },
    });

    if (!item) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Inventory item not found');
    }

    const created = await this.prisma.repriceFlowItem.create({
      data: {
        inventoryItemId,
        currentPrice: item.expectedSalePriceManual ?? item.totalCost,
        suggestedPrice: item.expectedSalePriceManual ?? item.totalCost,
        status: 'queued',
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: true,
      },
    });

    this.events.repriceChanged();

    return created;
  }

  async updateRepriceFlowStatus(id: string, status: string): Promise<unknown> {
    const updated = await this.prisma.repriceFlowItem.update({
      where: {
        id,
      },
      data: {
        status,
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: true,
      },
    });

    this.events.repriceChanged();

    return updated;
  }

  async getReviewFlow(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.reviewFlowItem.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: {
          include: {
            item: true,
            assignedUser: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });
  }

  async addToReviewFlow(
    inventoryItemId: string,
    reason?: string,
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    const item = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: inventoryItemId,
      },
    });

    if (!item) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Inventory item not found');
    }

    const created = await this.prisma.reviewFlowItem.create({
      data: {
        inventoryItemId,
        status: 'queued',
        reason,
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: true,
      },
    });

    this.events.reviewChanged();

        status,

import {
  BadRequestException,
  Body,
  Controller,
  Get,
  NotFoundException,
  Patch,
  Post,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { FlowsEventsService } from './flows-events.service';

@Controller('flows/purchase')
export class PurchaseFlowController {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly events: FlowsEventsService,
  ) {}

  @Get()
  async getItems(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.purchaseFlowItem.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'asc',
      },
      include: {
        watchlistItem: {
          include: {
            item: true,
            assignedUser: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });
  }

  @Post('add')
  async add(
    @Body()
    body: {
      watchlistItemId: string;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!body.watchlistItemId) {
      throw new BadRequestException('watchlistItemId is required');
    }

    const watchlistItem = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.watchlistItemId,
      },
    });

    if (!watchlistItem) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Watchlist item not found');
    }

    const created = await this.prisma.purchaseFlowItem.create({
      data: {
        watchlistItemId: body.watchlistItemId,
        selectedPrice: watchlistItem.desiredBuyPrice,
        selectedSource: 'manual',
        status: 'pending',
      },
      include: {
        watchlistItem: true,
      },
    });

    this.events.purchaseChanged();

    return created;
  }

  @Patch('mark-bought')
  async markBought(
    @Body()
    body: {
      id: string;
      purchasePrice: number;
      quantity: number;
      extraCosts?: number;
      condition?: string;
      completenessPercent?: number;
      sealed?: boolean;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!body.id) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Flow id is required');
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(body.purchasePrice) || body.purchasePrice <= 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException('purchasePrice must be greater than zero');
    }

    if (!Number.isInteger(body.quantity) || body.quantity <= 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException('quantity must be a positive integer');
    }

    const flowItem = await this.prisma.purchaseFlowItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.id,
      },
      include: {
        watchlistItem: true,
      },
    });

    if (!flowItem) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Purchase flow item not found');
    }

    const extraCosts = body.extraCosts ?? 0;
    const totalCost = body.purchasePrice + extraCosts;

    const result = await this.prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const inventoryItem = await tx.inventoryItem.create({
        data: {
          itemId: flowItem.watchlistItem.itemId,
          titleSnapshot: flowItem.watchlistItem.titleSnapshot,
          purchasePrice: body.purchasePrice,
          extraCosts,
          totalCost,
          quantity: body.quantity,
          condition: body.condition ?? 'used',
          completenessPercent: body.completenessPercent ?? 100,
          sealed: body.sealed ?? false,
          expectedSalePriceManual: flowItem.watchlistItem.targetSellPrice ?? null,
        },
        include: {
          item: true,
        },
      });

      const updatedFlow = await tx.purchaseFlowItem.update({
        where: {
          id: body.id,
        },
        data: {
          selectedPrice: body.purchasePrice,
          status: 'bought',
        },
        include: {
          watchlistItem: true,
        },
      });

      return {
        inventoryItem,
        flowItem: updatedFlow,
      };
    });

    this.events.purchaseChanged();
    this.events.itemChanged(flowItem.watchlistItem.itemId);
    this.events.opportunitiesChanged();

    return result;
  }

  @Patch('remove')
  async remove(
    @Body()
    body: {
      id: string;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!body.id) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Flow id is required');
    }

    const existing = await this.prisma.purchaseFlowItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.id,
      },
    });

    if (!existing) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Purchase flow item not found');
    }

    const deleted = await this.prisma.purchaseFlowItem.delete({
      where: {
        id: body.id,
      },
    });

    this.events.purchaseChanged();

    return deleted;
  }
}

import {
  BadRequestException,
  Body,
  Controller,
  Get,
  NotFoundException,
  Patch,
  Post,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { FlowsEventsService } from './flows-events.service';

@Controller('flows/reprice')
export class RepriceFlowController {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly events: FlowsEventsService,
  ) {}

  @Get()
  async getItems(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.repriceFlowItem.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'asc',
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: {
          include: {
            item: true,
            assignedUser: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });
  }

  @Post('add')
  async add(
    @Body()
    body: {
      inventoryItemId: string;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!body.inventoryItemId) {
      throw new BadRequestException('inventoryItemId is required');
    }

    const inventoryItem = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.inventoryItemId,
      },
    });

    if (!inventoryItem) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Inventory item not found');
    }

    const created = await this.prisma.repriceFlowItem.create({
      data: {
        inventoryItemId: body.inventoryItemId,
        currentPrice: inventoryItem.expectedSalePriceManual ?? inventoryItem.totalCost,
        suggestedPrice: inventoryItem.expectedSalePriceManual ?? inventoryItem.totalCost,
        status: 'pending',
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: true,
      },
    });

    this.events.repriceChanged();

    return created;
  }

  @Patch('mark-listed')
  async markListed(
    @Body()
    body: {
      id: string;
      price: number;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!body.id) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Flow id is required');
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(body.price) || body.price <= 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException('price must be greater than zero');
    }

    const flowItem = await this.prisma.repriceFlowItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.id,
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: true,
      },
    });

    if (!flowItem) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Reprice flow item not found');
    }

    const result = await this.prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const inventoryItem = await tx.inventoryItem.update({
        where: {
          id: flowItem.inventoryItemId,
        },
        data: {
          expectedSalePriceManual: body.price,
        },
      });

      const updatedFlow = await tx.repriceFlowItem.update({
        where: {
          id: body.id,
        },
        data: {
          currentPrice: body.price,
          suggestedPrice: body.price,
          status: 'listed',
        },
      });

      return {
        inventoryItem,
        flowItem: updatedFlow,
      };
    });

    this.events.repriceChanged();
    this.events.itemChanged(flowItem.inventoryItem.itemId);
    this.events.opportunitiesChanged();

    return result;
  }

  @Patch('remove')
  async remove(
    @Body()
    body: {
      id: string;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!body.id) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Flow id is required');
    }

    const existing = await this.prisma.repriceFlowItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.id,
      },
    });

    if (!existing) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Reprice flow item not found');
    }

    const deleted = await this.prisma.repriceFlowItem.delete({
      where: {
        id: body.id,
      },
    });

    this.events.repriceChanged();

    return deleted;
  }
}

  Body,

  Get,
  NotFoundException,
  Patch,
  Post,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { FlowsEventsService } from './flows-events.service';

@Controller('flows/review')
export class ReviewFlowController {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly events: FlowsEventsService,
  ) {}

  @Get()
  async getItems(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.reviewFlowItem.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'asc',
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: {
          include: {
            item: true,
            assignedUser: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });
  }

  @Post('add')
  async add(
    @Body()
    body: {
      inventoryItemId: string;
      reason?: string;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!body.inventoryItemId) {
      throw new BadRequestException('inventoryItemId is required');
    }

    const inventoryItem = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.inventoryItemId,
      },
    });

    if (!inventoryItem) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Inventory item not found');
    }

    const created = await this.prisma.reviewFlowItem.create({
      data: {
        inventoryItemId: body.inventoryItemId,
        reason: body.reason,
        status: 'pending',
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: true,
      },
    });

    this.events.reviewChanged();

    return created;
  }

  @Patch('mark-reviewed')
  async markReviewed(
    @Body()
    body: {
      id: string;
      note?: string;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!body.id) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Flow id is required');
    }

    const flowItem = await this.prisma.reviewFlowItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.id,
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: true,
      },
    });

    if (!flowItem) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Review flow item not found');
    }

    const updated = await this.prisma.reviewFlowItem.update({
      where: {
        id: body.id,
      },
      data: {
        status: 'reviewed',
        reason: body.note ?? flowItem.reason,
      },
    });

    this.events.reviewChanged();
    this.events.itemChanged(flowItem.inventoryItem.itemId);

    return updated;
  }

  @Patch('remove')
  async remove(
    @Body()
    body: {
      id: string;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!body.id) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Flow id is required');
    }

    const existing = await this.prisma.reviewFlowItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.id,
      },
    });

    if (!existing) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Review flow item not found');
    }

    const deleted = await this.prisma.reviewFlowItem.delete({
      where: {
        id: body.id,
      },
    });

    this.events.reviewChanged();

    return deleted;
  }
}

import { Controller, Get, Param, Query } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InsightsService } from './insights.service';

@Controller('insights')
export class InsightsController {
  constructor(private readonly insightsService: InsightsService) {}

  @Get('inventory/:inventoryItemId')
  async getInventoryInsight(
    @Param('inventoryItemId') inventoryItemId: string,
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.insightsService.getInventoryInsight(inventoryItemId);
  }

  @Get('watchlist/:watchlistItemId')
  async getWatchlistInsight(
    @Param('watchlistItemId') watchlistItemId: string,
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.insightsService.getWatchlistInsight(watchlistItemId);
  }

  @Get('dashboard')
  async getDashboardInsights(
    @Query('limit') limit?: string,
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.insightsService.getDashboardInsights(limit ? Number(limit) : 20);
  }
}

import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';
