===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\planning\planning.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { DailyPlanService } from '../strategy/daily-plan.service';

@Injectable()
export class PlanningService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly dailyPlanService: DailyPlanService,
  ) {}

  async getDailyPlan(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const [purchasePending, repricePending, reviewPending, unresolvedPending] =
      await Promise.all([
        this.prisma.purchaseFlowItem.count({ where: { status: 'pending' } }),
        this.prisma.repriceFlowItem.count({ where: { status: 'pending' } }),
        this.prisma.reviewFlowItem.count({ where: { status: 'pending' } }),
        this.prisma.unresolvedMatchQueue.count({ where: { status: 'pending' } }),
      ]);

    const sources = await this.prisma.marketSource.findMany({
      select: { id: true },
    });

    let staleSources = 0;

    for (const source of sources) {
      const latestListing = await this.prisma.marketListing.findFirst({
        where: { sourceId: source.id },
        orderBy: { fetchedAt: 'desc' },
        select: { fetchedAt: true },
      });

      if (!latestListing) {
        staleSources += 1;
        continue;
      }

      const diffHours =
        (Date.now() - latestListing.fetchedAt.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60);

      if (diffHours > 24) {
        staleSources += 1;
      }
    }

    return this.dailyPlanService.generate({
      buyCount: purchasePending,
      sellCount: repricePending,
      reviewCount: reviewPending,
      unresolvedCount: unresolvedPending,
      staleSources,
    });
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\pricing\pricing.controller.ts =====

import { Body, Controller, Post } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PricingService } from './pricing.service';

@Controller('pricing')
export class PricingController {
  constructor(private readonly pricing: PricingService) {}

  @Post('analyze')
  analyze(
    @Body()
    body: {
      buyPrice: number;
      sellPrice?: number;
    },
  ) {
    const sell =
      body.sellPrice ??
      this.pricing.suggestSellPrice({
        buyPrice: body.buyPrice,
      });

    const roi = this.pricing.calculateROI({
      buyPrice: body.buyPrice,
      sellPrice: sell,
    });

    const profit = this.pricing.calculateProfit({
      buyPrice: body.buyPrice,
      sellPrice: sell,
    });

    const classification = this.pricing.classifyDeal({ roi });

    return {
      suggestedSellPrice: sell,
      roi,
      profit,
      classification,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\pricing\pricing.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  calculateProfit,
  calculateRoiPercent,
  toMoney,
} from '../../common/money.utils';

@Injectable()
export class PricingService {
  calculateROI(params: {
    buyPrice: number;
    sellPrice: number;
  }): number {
    const profit = calculateProfit({
      revenue: params.sellPrice,
      cost: params.buyPrice,
    });

    return calculateRoiPercent({
      profit,
      cost: params.buyPrice,
    });
  }

  calculateProfit(params: {
    buyPrice: number;
    sellPrice: number;
  }): number {
    return calculateProfit({
      revenue: params.sellPrice,
      cost: params.buyPrice,
    });
  }

  suggestSellPrice(params: {
    buyPrice: number;
    targetRoi?: number;
  }): number {
    const roi = params.targetRoi ?? 40;
    return toMoney(params.buyPrice * (1 + roi / 100));
  }

  classifyDeal(params: {
    roi: number;
  }): 'bad' | 'ok' | 'good' | 'insane' {
    if (params.roi < 10) return 'bad';
    if (params.roi < 30) return 'ok';
    if (params.roi < 60) return 'good';
    return 'insane';
  }
}

import { INestApplication, Injectable, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';

@Injectable()
export class PrismaService extends PrismaClient implements OnModuleInit {
  async onModuleInit(): Promise<void> {
    await this.$connect();
  }

  async enableShutdownHooks(app: INestApplication): Promise<void> {
    process.on('beforeExit', async () => {
      await app.close();
    });
  }
}

import { PublicService } from './public.service';

@Controller('public')
export class PublicController {
  constructor(private readonly publicService: PublicService) {}

  @Get('catalog')
  async getCatalog(
    @Query('q') q?: string,
    @Query('type') type?: string,
    @Query('theme') theme?: string,
    @Query('sort') sort?: string,
    @Query('availableOnly') availableOnly?: string,
    @Query('limit') limit?: string,
  ): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.publicService.getCatalog({
      q,
      type,
      theme,
      sort,
      availableOnly: availableOnly === 'true',
      limit: limit ? Number(limit) : 48,
    });
  }

  @Get('catalog/:slug')
  async getCatalogItemBySlug(
    @Param('slug') slug: string,
  ): Promise<unknown | null> {
    return this.publicService.getCatalogItemBySlug(slug);
  }

  @Post('reserve')
  async createReserveRequest(
    @Body()
    body: {
      inventoryItemId?: string;
      productTitle?: string;
      name: string;
      contact: string;
      message?: string;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.publicService.createReserveRequest(body);
  }

  @Get('reserve-requests')
  async getReserveRequests(
    @Query('q') q?: string,
    @Query('status') status?: string,
  ): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.publicService.getReserveRequests({
      q,
      status,
    });
  }

  @Patch('reserve-requests')
  async updateReserveRequest(
    @Body()
    body: {
      id: string;
      status?: string;
      adminNote?: string | null;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.publicService.updateReserveRequest(body);
  }

  @Get('reserve-board')
  async getReserveBoard(): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.publicService.getReserveBoard();
  }

  @Get('analytics')
  async getStoreAnalytics(): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.publicService.getStoreAnalytics();

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\public\public.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';

@Injectable()
export class PublicService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  async getCatalog(params: {
    q?: string;
    type?: string;
    availableOnly?: boolean;
    theme?: string;
    sort?: string;
    limit?: number;
  }): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const limit = params.limit ?? 48;
    const q = params.q?.trim();
    const orderBy =
      params.sort === 'price_asc'
        ? { expectedSalePriceManual: 'asc' as const }
        : params.sort === 'price_desc'
          ? { expectedSalePriceManual: 'desc' as const }
          : params.sort === 'title_asc'
            ? { titleSnapshot: 'asc' as const }
            : { createdAt: 'desc' as const };

    return this.prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
      where: {
        quantity: params.availableOnly === true ? { gt: 0 } : undefined,
        OR:
          q && q.length > 0
            ? [
                {
                  titleSnapshot: {
                    contains: q,
                    mode: 'insensitive',
                  },
                },
                {
                  item: {
                    setNumber: {
                      contains: q,
                      mode: 'insensitive',
                    },
                  },
                },
                {
                  item: {
                    theme: {
                      contains: q,
                      mode: 'insensitive',
                    },
                  },
                },
              ]
            : undefined,
        item: {
          ...(params.type ? { kind: params.type } : {}),
          ...(params.theme
            ? {
                theme: {
                  equals: params.theme,
                  mode: 'insensitive',
                },
              }
            : {}),
        },
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
        images: {
          orderBy: {
            sortOrder: 'asc',
          },
        },
      },
      orderBy,
      take: limit,
    });
  }

  async getCatalogItemBySlug(slug: string): Promise<unknown | null> {
    const all = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
      where: {
        quantity: { gt: 0 },
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
        images: {
          orderBy: {
            sortOrder: 'asc',
          },
        },
      },
      take: 200,
    });

    const normalized = slug.trim().toLowerCase();
    const found = all.find((entry) => {
      const title = (entry.titleSnapshot || entry.item?.title || '')
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase()
        .replaceAll(' ', '-');
      return title === normalized;
    });

    return found ?? null;
  }

  async createReserveRequest(body: {
    inventoryItemId?: string;
    productTitle?: string;
    name: string;
    contact: string;
    message?: string;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.prisma.reserveRequest.create({
      data: {
        inventoryItemId: body.inventoryItemId,
        productTitle: body.productTitle ?? '',
        name: body.name,
        contact: body.contact,
        message: body.message ?? '',
        status: 'pending',
      },
    });
  }

  async getReserveRequests(params?: {
    q?: string;
    status?: string;
  }): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.reserveRequest.findMany({
      where: {
        ...(params?.status && params.status !== 'all'
          ? { status: params.status }
          : {}),
        ...(params?.q
          ? {
              OR: [
                {
                  productTitle: {
                    contains: params.q,
                    mode: 'insensitive',
                  },
                },
                {
                  name: {
                    contains: params.q,
                    mode: 'insensitive',
                  },
                },
                {
                  contact: {
                    contains: params.q,
                    mode: 'insensitive',
                  },
                },
              ],
            }
          : {}),
      },
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
    });
  }

  async updateReserveRequest(body: {
    id: string;
    status?: string;
    adminNote?: string | null;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.prisma.reserveRequest.update({
      where: { id: body.id },
      data: {
        status: body.status,
        adminNote: body.adminNote,
      },
    });
  }

  async getReserveBoard(): Promise<unknown> {
    const rows = await this.prisma.reserveRequest.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
    });

    return {
      pending: rows.filter((x) => x.status === 'pending'),
      approved: rows.filter((x) => x.status === 'approved'),
      contacted: rows.filter((x) => x.status === 'contacted'),
      rejected: rows.filter((x) => x.status === 'rejected'),
    };
  }

  async getStoreAnalytics(): Promise<unknown> {
    const inventory = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findMany();
    const reserves = await this.prisma.reserveRequest.findMany();
    const available = inventory.filter((x) => x.quantity > 0);
    const avgPrice =
      available.length > 0
        ? available.reduce(
            (sum, x) => sum + Number(x.expectedSalePriceManual ?? x.totalCost ?? 0),
            0,
          ) / available.length
        : 0;

    return {
      totalInventory: inventory.length,
      availableInventory: available.length,
      reserveRequests: reserves.length,
      pendingReserves: reserves.filter((x) => x.status === 'pending').length,
      avgVisiblePrice: Number(avgPrice.toFixed(2)),
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\realtime\realtime.gateway.ts =====

import {
  OnGatewayConnection,
  OnGatewayDisconnect,
  WebSocketGateway,
  WebSocketServer,
} from '@nestjs/websockets';
import { Server, Socket } from 'socket.io';

type RefreshPayload = {
  type: string;
  timestamp: number;
  payload?: unknown;
};

@WebSocketGateway({
  cors: {
    origin: '*',
  },
})
export class RealtimeGateway implements OnGatewayConnection, OnGatewayDisconnect {
  @WebSocketServer()
  server!: Server;

  private clients = new Set<string>();

  handleConnection(client: Socket): void {
    this.clients.add(client.id);

    client.emit('connected', {
      message: 'Realtime connected',
      clientId: client.id,
    });
  }

  handleDisconnect(client: Socket): void {
    this.clients.delete(client.id);
  }

  private buildPayload(type: string, payload?: unknown): RefreshPayload {
    return {
      type,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      payload,
    };
  }

  private emit(event: string, payload: RefreshPayload): void {
    if (!this.server) {
      return;
    }

    this.server.emit(event, payload);
  }

  emitEvent(event: string, payload?: unknown): void {
    this.emit(event, this.buildPayload(event, payload));
  }

  emitCustom(event: string, payload?: unknown): void {
    this.emit(event, this.buildPayload(event, payload));
  }

  emitNotification(payload: unknown): void {
    this.emit('notification', this.buildPayload('notification', payload));
  }

  emitInventoryUpdated(payload: unknown): void {
    this.emit('inventory_updated', this.buildPayload('inventory_updated', payload));
    this.emitInventoryRefresh(payload);
  }

  emitWatchlistUpdated(payload: unknown): void {
    this.emit('watchlist_updated', this.buildPayload('watchlist_updated', payload));
    this.emitWatchlistRefresh(payload);
  }

  emitSaleRegistered(payload: unknown): void {
    this.emit('sale_registered', this.buildPayload('sale_registered', payload));
    this.emitDashboardRefresh('sale_registered');
  }

  emitFlowRefresh(flow?: string): void {
    this.emit(
      'flow.refresh',
      this.buildPayload('flow.refresh', {
        flow,
      }),
    );
  }

  emitDashboardRefresh(reason?: string): void {
    this.emit(
      'dashboard.refresh',
      this.buildPayload('dashboard.refresh', {
        reason,
      }),
    );
  }

  emitItemRefresh(itemId?: string, reason?: string): void {
    this.emit(
      'item.refresh',
      this.buildPayload('item.refresh', {
        itemId,
        reason,
      }),
    );
  }

  emitOpportunityRefresh(scope?: string): void {
    this.emit(
      'opportunity.refresh',
      this.buildPayload('opportunity.refresh', {
        scope,
      }),
    );
  }

  emitWatchlistRefresh(payload?: unknown): void {
    this.emit('watchlist.refresh', this.buildPayload('watchlist.refresh', payload));
  }

  emitInventoryRefresh(payload?: unknown): void {
    this.emit('inventory.refresh', this.buildPayload('inventory.refresh', payload));
  }

  emitListingsRefresh(payload?: unknown): void {
    this.emit('listings.refresh', this.buildPayload('listings.refresh', payload));
  }

  getConnectedClientsCount(): number {
    return this.clients.size;
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\repricer\repricer.service.ts =====

import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  calculateRoiPercent,
  toMoney,
} from '../../common/money.utils';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';

@Injectable()
export class RepricerService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  calculateSuggestedPrice(params: {
    totalCost: number;
    marketFloor?: number | null;
    marketAverage?: number | null;
    marketCeiling?: number | null;
    targetRoiPercent?: number | null;
  }): number {
    const targetRoi = params.targetRoiPercent ?? 40;
    const roiBased = params.totalCost * (1 + targetRoi / 100);

    const floor = params.marketFloor ?? null;
    const avg = params.marketAverage ?? null;
    const ceiling = params.marketCeiling ?? null;

    let suggested = roiBased;

    if (floor != null && avg != null) {
      suggested = Math.max(roiBased, Math.min(avg, ceiling ?? avg));
      suggested = Math.max(suggested, floor);
    } else if (avg != null) {
      suggested = Math.max(roiBased, avg);
    } else if (floor != null) {
      suggested = Math.max(roiBased, floor);
    }

    return Math.round(toMoney(suggested));
  }

  classifyPricing(params: {
    suggestedPrice: number;
    marketFloor?: number | null;
    marketAverage?: number | null;
  }): 'aggressive' | 'balanced' | 'premium' {
    const floor = params.marketFloor ?? null;
    const avg = params.marketAverage ?? null;

    if (floor != null && params.suggestedPrice <= floor) {
      return 'aggressive';
    }

    if (avg != null && params.suggestedPrice > avg * 1.08) {
      return 'premium';
    }

    return 'balanced';
  }

  async analyzeInventoryItem(body: {
    inventoryItemId: string;
    marketFloor?: number | null;
    marketAverage?: number | null;
    marketCeiling?: number | null;
    targetRoiPercent?: number | null;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    const item = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.inventoryItemId,
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
      },
    });

    if (!item) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Inventory item not found');
    }

    const totalCost = toMoney(item.totalCost ?? item.purchasePrice ?? 0);

    const suggestedPrice = this.calculateSuggestedPrice({
      totalCost,
      marketFloor: body.marketFloor,
      marketAverage: body.marketAverage,
      marketCeiling: body.marketCeiling,
      targetRoiPercent: body.targetRoiPercent,
    });

    const roiPercent = calculateRoiPercent({
      profit: suggestedPrice - totalCost,
      cost: totalCost,
    });

    const classification = this.classifyPricing({
      suggestedPrice,
      marketFloor: body.marketFloor,
      marketAverage: body.marketAverage,
    });

    return {
      inventoryItemId: item.id,
      title: item.titleSnapshot,
      totalCost,
      marketFloor: body.marketFloor ?? null,
      marketAverage: body.marketAverage ?? null,
      marketCeiling: body.marketCeiling ?? null,
      suggestedPrice,
      roiPercent,
      classification,
    };
  }

  async analyzeFromSoldComps(body: {
    inventoryItemId: string;
    targetRoiPercent?: number | null;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    const inventory = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.inventoryItemId,
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
      },
    });

    if (!inventory) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Inventory item not found');
    }

    const soldComps = await this.prisma.soldComp.findMany({
      where: inventory.item?.setNumber
        ? {
            extractedSetNo: inventory.item.setNumber,
          }
        : {
            normalizedTitle: {
              contains: inventory.titleSnapshot.toLowerCase().slice(0, 18),
              mode: 'insensitive',
            },
          },
      orderBy: [
        {
          soldAt: 'desc',
        },
        {
          createdAt: 'desc',
        },
      ],
      take: 30,
    });

    if (soldComps.length === 0) {
      return {
        inventoryItemId: inventory.id,
        title: inventory.titleSnapshot,
        soldCompCount: 0,
        marketFloor: null,
        marketAverage: null,
        marketCeiling: null,
        suggestedPrice: null,
        roiPercent: null,
        classification: null,
      };
    }

    const prices = soldComps
      .map((comp) => Number(comp.soldPrice))
      .filter((value) => Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0);

    const marketFloor = Math.min(...prices);
    const marketCeiling = Math.max(...prices);
    const marketAverage =
      prices.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / prices.length;

    const totalCost = toMoney(inventory.totalCost ?? inventory.purchasePrice ?? 0);

    const suggestedPrice = this.calculateSuggestedPrice({
      totalCost,
      marketFloor,
      marketAverage,
      marketCeiling,
      targetRoiPercent: body.targetRoiPercent,
    });

    const roiPercent = calculateRoiPercent({
      profit: suggestedPrice - totalCost,
      cost: totalCost,
    });

    const classification = this.classifyPricing({
      suggestedPrice,
      marketFloor,
      marketAverage,
    });

    return {
      inventoryItemId: inventory.id,
      title: inventory.titleSnapshot,
      soldCompCount: soldComps.length,
      marketFloor: toMoney(marketFloor),
      marketAverage: toMoney(marketAverage),
      marketCeiling: toMoney(marketCeiling),
      suggestedPrice,
      roiPercent,
      classification,
    };
  }

  async applySuggestedPrice(body: {
    inventoryItemId: string;
    suggestedPrice: number;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    const price = toMoney(body.suggestedPrice);

    return this.prisma.inventoryItem.update({
      where: {
        id: body.inventoryItemId,
      },
      data: {
        expectedSalePriceManual: price,
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
        images: {
          orderBy: {
            sortOrder: 'asc',
          },
        },
      },
    });
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\sales\sales.service.ts =====

import {
  BadRequestException,
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  calculateProfit,
  toMoney,
} from '../../common/money.utils';
import { ActivityService } from '../activity/activity.service';
import { NotificationsService } from '../notifications/notifications.service';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { RealtimeGateway } from '../realtime/realtime.gateway';

@Injectable()
export class SalesService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly notifications: NotificationsService,
    private readonly realtime: RealtimeGateway,
    private readonly activity: ActivityService,
  ) {}

  async registerSale(body: {
    inventoryItemId: string;
    sellPrice: number;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!body.inventoryItemId) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Missing inventoryItemId');
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(body.sellPrice) || body.sellPrice <= 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException('sellPrice must be greater than zero');
    }

    const item = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.inventoryItemId,
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
      },
    });

    if (!item) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Inventory item not found');
    }

    if (item.quantity <= 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Inventory item is out of stock');
    }

    const unitCost = toMoney(item.totalCost / item.quantity);
    const sellPrice = toMoney(body.sellPrice);

    const profit = calculateProfit({
      revenue: sellPrice,
      cost: unitCost,
    });

    const sale = await this.prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.inventoryItem.update({
        where: {
          id: item.id,
        },
        data: {
          quantity: Math.max(0, item.quantity - 1),
        },
      });

      return tx.sale.create({
        data: {
          inventoryItemId: item.id,
          sellPrice,
          profit,
        },
        include: {
          inventoryItem: {
            include: {
              item: true,
            },
          },
        },
      });
    });

    await this.activity.log('sale.registered', {
      inventoryItemId: item.id,
      itemId: item.itemId,
      title: item.titleSnapshot,
      sellPrice,
      unitCost,
      profit,
    });

    await this.notifications.createSaleNotification({
      itemTitle: item.titleSnapshot,
      profit,
    });

    this.realtime.emitSaleRegistered({
      inventoryItemId: item.id,
      itemId: item.itemId,
      title: item.titleSnapshot,
      profit,
      sellPrice,
    });

    this.realtime.emitInventoryUpdated({
      id: item.id,
      itemId: item.itemId,
      quantity: Math.max(0, item.quantity - 1),
    });

    return sale;
  }

  async getSales(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.sale.findMany({
      include: {
        inventoryItem: {
          include: {
            item: true,
          },
        },
      },
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
    });
  }

  async getProfitStats(): Promise<{
    totalProfit: number;
    totalRevenue: number;
    salesCount: number;
    recentSales: unknown[];
  }> {
    const sales = await this.prisma.sale.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
      include: {
        inventoryItem: {
          include: {
            item: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });

    const totalProfit = toMoney(
      sales.reduce((sum, sale) => sum + sale.profit, 0),
    );

    const totalRevenue = toMoney(
      sales.reduce((sum, sale) => sum + sale.sellPrice, 0),
    );

    return {
      totalProfit,
      totalRevenue,
      salesCount: sales.length,
      recentSales: sales.slice(0, 20),
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\scanner\scanner-executor.service.ts =====

import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { ScannerService } from './scanner.service';

@Injectable()
export class ScannerExecutorService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly scanner: ScannerService,
  ) {}

  async runJob(body: {
    jobId: string;
    listings?: Array<{
      externalId: string;
      title: string;
      price: number;
      url?: string | null;
      imageUrl?: string | null;
    }>;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    const job = await this.prisma.scanJob.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: body.jobId,
      },
    });

    if (!job) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Scan job not found');
    }

    await this.prisma.scanJob.update({
      where: {
        id: job.id,
      },
      data: {
        status: 'running',
        startedAt: new Date(),
      },
    });

    const listings = await this.scanner.ingestListings({
      sourceCode: job.sourceCode,
      listings: body.listings ?? [],
    });

    return this.prisma.scanJob.update({
      where: {
        id: job.id,
      },
      data: {
        status: 'completed',
        finishedAt: new Date(),
        resultCount: listings.length,
      },
    });
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\scanner\scanner.service.ts =====

import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';

@Injectable()
export class ScannerService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  normalizeTitle(title: string): string {
    return title
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s-]+/gu, ' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim();
  }

  extractSetNumber(title: string): string | null {
    const match = title.match(/\b\d{4,7}\b/);
    return match?.[0] ?? null;
  }

  private createListingId(sourceCode: string, externalId: string): string {
    const encoded = Buffer.from(externalId)
      .toString('base64')
      .replaceAll('=', '')
      .replaceAll('+', '-')
      .replaceAll('/', '_');

    return `${sourceCode}_${encoded}`;
  }

  private async resolveItemId(title: string): Promise<string> {
    const setNumber = this.extractSetNumber(title);

    if (setNumber) {
      const item = await this.prisma.item.findFirst({
        where: { setNumber },
        select: { id: true },
      });

      if (item) {
        return item.id;
      }
    }

    const placeholder = await this.prisma.item.upsert({
      where: { id: 'item_unresolved_placeholder' },
      update: {},
      create: {
        id: 'item_unresolved_placeholder',
        kind: 'unknown',
        title: 'UNRESOLVED_PLACEHOLDER',
        conditionDefault: 'unknown',
      },
    });

    return placeholder.id;
  }

  async createSource(body: {
    code: string;
    name: string;
    type?: string | null;
    enabled?: boolean | null;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.prisma.marketSource.upsert({
      where: { code: body.code },
      update: {
        name: body.name,
        type: body.type ?? 'manual',
        enabled: body.enabled ?? true,
      },
      create: {
        code: body.code,
        name: body.name,
        type: body.type ?? 'manual',
        enabled: body.enabled ?? true,
      },
    });
  }

  async getSources(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.marketSource.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'asc',
      },
    });
  }

  async enqueueScan(body: {
    sourceCode: string;
    query?: string | null;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    const source = await this.prisma.marketSource.findUnique({
      where: {
        code: body.sourceCode,
      },
    });

    if (!source) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Source not found');
    }

    return this.prisma.scanJob.create({
      data: {
        sourceCode: body.sourceCode,
        query: body.query ?? '',
        status: 'queued',
      },
    });
  }

  async getJobs(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.scanJob.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
    });
  }

  async ingestListings(body: {
    sourceCode: string;
    listings: Array<{
      externalId: string;
      title: string;
      price: number;
      url?: string | null;
      imageUrl?: string | null;
    }>;
  }): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const source = await this.prisma.marketSource.findUnique({
      where: { code: body.sourceCode },
    });

    if (!source) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Source not found');
    }

    const rows: unknown[] = [];

    for (const listing of body.listings) {
      const id = this.createListingId(body.sourceCode, listing.externalId);
      const itemId = await this.resolveItemId(listing.title);

      const upserted = await this.prisma.marketListing.upsert({
        where: { id },
        update: {
          itemId,
          titleRaw: listing.title,
          url: listing.url ?? '',
          price: listing.price,
          currency: 'UAH',
          status: 'active',
          fetchedAt: new Date(),
          lastSeenAt: new Date(),
        },
        create: {
          id,
          sourceId: source.id,
          itemId,
          externalListingId: listing.externalId,
          titleRaw: listing.title,
          url: listing.url ?? '',
          price: listing.price,
          currency: 'UAH',
          status: 'active',
          fetchedAt: new Date(),
        },
        include: {
          source: true,
          item: true,
        },
      });

      rows.push(upserted);
    }

    return rows;
  }

  async getListings(params?: {
    sourceCode?: string;
    limit?: number;
  }): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const source = params?.sourceCode
      ? await this.prisma.marketSource.findUnique({
          where: { code: params.sourceCode },
        })
      : null;

    return this.prisma.marketListing.findMany({
      where: source
        ? {
            sourceId: source.id,
          }
        : undefined,
      orderBy: {
        fetchedAt: 'desc',
      },
      take: params?.limit ?? 100,
      include: {
        source: true,
        item: true,
      },
    });
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\source-health\source-health.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';

type SourceHealthRow = {
  sourceCode: string;
  sourceName: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  listingCount: number;
  latestRunStatus: string;
  latestRunStartedAt: Date | null;
  latestRunFinishedAt: Date | null;
  latestRunItemsSeen: number;
  latestRunItemsMatched: number;
  latestRunId: string | null;
  freshnessLabel: string;
  latestErrorMessage: string | null;
};

@Injectable()
export class SourceHealthService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  private getFreshnessLabel(date: Date | null): string {
    if (!date) {
      return 'missing';
    }

    const diffHours = (Date.now() - date.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60);

    if (diffHours <= 6) return 'fresh';
    if (diffHours <= 24) return 'recent';
    if (diffHours <= 72) return 'aging';

    return 'stale';
  }

  async getSourceHealthSummary(): Promise<SourceHealthRow[]> {
    const sources = await this.prisma.marketSource.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        code: 'asc',
      },
    });

    const result: SourceHealthRow[] = [];

    for (const source of sources) {
      const [latestRun, latestListing, listingCount, latestError] =
        await Promise.all([
          this.prisma.sourceRunLog.findFirst({
            where: {
              sourceId: source.id,
            },
            orderBy: {
              startedAt: 'desc',
            },
          }),
          this.prisma.marketListing.findFirst({
            where: {
              sourceId: source.id,
            },
            orderBy: {
              fetchedAt: 'desc',
            },
          }),
          this.prisma.marketListing.count({
            where: {
              sourceId: source.id,
            },
          }),
          this.prisma.syncErrorLog.findFirst({
            where: {
              sourceCode: source.code,
            },
            orderBy: {
              createdAt: 'desc',
            },
          }),
        ]);

      result.push({
        sourceCode: source.code,
        sourceName: source.name,
        enabled: source.enabled,
        listingCount,
        latestRunStatus: latestRun?.status ?? 'never',
        latestRunStartedAt: latestRun?.startedAt ?? null,
        latestRunFinishedAt: latestRun?.finishedAt ?? null,
        latestRunItemsSeen: latestRun?.itemsSeen ?? 0,
        latestRunItemsMatched: latestRun?.itemsMatched ?? 0,
        latestRunId: latestRun?.id ?? null,
        freshnessLabel: this.getFreshnessLabel(latestListing?.fetchedAt ?? null),
        latestErrorMessage: latestError?.message ?? null,
      });
    }

    return result;
  }

  async getRecentRunHistory(limit = 30): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.sourceRunLog.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        startedAt: 'desc',
      },
      take: limit,
      include: {
        source: true,
      },
    });
  }

  async getRunById(runId: string): Promise<unknown | null> {
    return this.prisma.sourceRunLog.findUnique({
      where: {
        id: runId,
      },
      include: {
        source: true,
      },
    });
  }

  async getRecentSyncErrors(
    limit = 50,
    sourceCode?: string,
  ): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.syncErrorLog.findMany({
      where: {
        sourceCode: sourceCode ?? undefined,
      },
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
      take: limit,
    });
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\source-health\source-rerun.service.ts =====

import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { SyncService } from '../sync/sync.service';

@Injectable()
export class SourceRerunService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly syncService: SyncService,
  ) {}

  async triggerSourceRerun(sourceCode: string): Promise<{
    sourceCode: string;
    accepted: boolean;
    message: string;
  }> {
    const source = await this.prisma.marketSource.findUnique({
      where: {
        code: sourceCode,
      },
    });

    if (!source) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Source not found');
    }

    await this.prisma.sourceRunLog.create({
      data: {
        sourceId: source.id,
        startedAt: new Date(),
        finishedAt: new Date(),
        status: 'queued_manual_rerun',
        itemsSeen: 0,
        itemsMatched: 0,
        itemsInserted: 0,
        itemsUpdated: 0,
      },
    });

    await this.syncService.logSyncError({
      scope: 'source_rerun',
      sourceCode,
      message: 'Manual source rerun was requested',
      detailsJson: {
        sourceId: source.id,
      },
    });

    return {
      sourceCode,
      accepted: true,
      message: 'Manual rerun request accepted',
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\bundle-breakup-estimator.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  addMoney,
  calculateRoiPercent,
  toMoney,
} from '../../common/money.utils';

export type BundleItem = {
  itemId: string;
  estimatedSell: number;
};

export type BundleBreakupInput = {
  bundlePrice: number;
  items: BundleItem[];
};

export type BundleBreakupResult = {
  totalResell: number;
  netAfterFees: number;
  roiPercent: number;
  recommendation: 'break_bundle' | 'keep_bundle' | 'avoid';
};

@Injectable()
export class BundleBreakupEstimatorService {
  estimate(input: BundleBreakupInput): BundleBreakupResult {
    const totalResell = addMoney(
      ...input.items.map((item) => item.estimatedSell),
    );

    const feeFactor = 0.12;
    const netAfterFees = toMoney(totalResell * (1 - feeFactor));
    const profit = toMoney(netAfterFees - input.bundlePrice);

    const roiPercent = calculateRoiPercent({
      profit,
      cost: input.bundlePrice,
    });

    let recommendation: BundleBreakupResult['recommendation'] = 'avoid';

    if (roiPercent >= 30) {
      recommendation = 'break_bundle';
    } else if (roiPercent >= 12) {
      recommendation = 'keep_bundle';
    }

    return {
      totalResell,
      netAfterFees,
      roiPercent,
      recommendation,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\bundle-detection.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';

export type BundleDetectionInput = {
  title: string;
  price: number;
};

export type BundleDetectionResult = {
  isBundle: boolean;
  unitCountEstimate: number;
  keywords: string[];
  confidence: number;
};

@Injectable()
export class BundleDetectionService {
  detect(input: BundleDetectionInput): BundleDetectionResult {
    const title = input.title.toLowerCase();
    const keywords: string[] = [];

    if (title.includes('lot')) keywords.push('lot');
    if (title.includes('bundle')) keywords.push('bundle');
    if (title.includes('collection')) keywords.push('collection');
    if (title.includes('x2')) keywords.push('x2');
    if (title.includes('x3')) keywords.push('x3');
    if (title.includes('x4')) keywords.push('x4');

    if (title.includes('РЅР°Р±С–СЂ')) keywords.push('РЅР°Р±С–СЂ');
    if (title.includes('РєРѕРјРїР»РµРєС‚')) keywords.push('РєРѕРјРїР»РµРєС‚');
    if (title.includes('РєРѕР»РµРєС†С–СЏ')) keywords.push('РєРѕР»РµРєС†С–СЏ');

    let unitCountEstimate = 1;

    if (title.includes('x2')) unitCountEstimate = 2;
    if (title.includes('x3')) unitCountEstimate = 3;
    if (title.includes('x4')) unitCountEstimate = 4;

    const isBundle = keywords.length > 0 || unitCountEstimate > 1;

    return {
      isBundle,
      unitCountEstimate,
      keywords,
      confidence: isBundle ? 0.78 : 0.22,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\capital-allocation.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { toMoney } from '../../common/money.utils';

export type CapitalAllocationCandidate = {
  itemId: string;
  title: string;
  buyPrice: number;
  expectedNetProfit: number;
  roiPercent: number;
  liquidityScore: number;
  confidenceScore: number;
  strategy: string;
};

export type CapitalAllocationResult = {
  itemId: string;
  title: string;
  allocation: number;
  units: number;
  reservedCapital: number;
  priorityScore: number;
  strategy: string;
};

@Injectable()
export class CapitalAllocationService {
  allocate(
    capital: number,
    candidates: CapitalAllocationCandidate[],
  ): CapitalAllocationResult[] {
    if (!Number.isFinite(capital) || capital <= 0 || candidates.length === 0) {
      return [];
    }

    const scored = candidates
      .filter((candidate) => Number.isFinite(candidate.buyPrice) && candidate.buyPrice > 0)
      .map((candidate) => {
        const priorityScore =
          candidate.expectedNetProfit * 0.35 +
          candidate.roiPercent * 0.25 +
          candidate.liquidityScore * 20 +
          candidate.confidenceScore * 20;

        return {
          ...candidate,
          priorityScore: Number(priorityScore.toFixed(2)),
        };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => b.priorityScore - a.priorityScore);

    let remaining = toMoney(capital);
    const output: CapitalAllocationResult[] = [];

    for (const candidate of scored) {
      if (remaining < candidate.buyPrice) {
        continue;
      }

      const maxShare =
        candidate.strategy === 'quick_flip'
          ? 0.35
          : candidate.strategy === 'hold_flip'
            ? 0.25
            : 0.2;

      const bucket = Math.min(remaining, capital * maxShare);
      const units = Math.max(1, Math.floor(bucket / candidate.buyPrice));
      const reservedCapital = toMoney(units * candidate.buyPrice);

      if (units <= 0 || reservedCapital > remaining) {
        continue;
      }

      output.push({
        itemId: candidate.itemId,
        title: candidate.title,
        allocation: Number((reservedCapital / capital).toFixed(4)),
        units,
        reservedCapital,
        priorityScore: candidate.priorityScore,
        strategy: candidate.strategy,
      });

      remaining = toMoney(remaining - reservedCapital);

      if (remaining <= 0) {
        break;
      }
    }

    return output;
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\daily-plan.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';

export type DailyPlanInput = {
  buyCount: number;
  sellCount: number;
  reviewCount: number;
  unresolvedCount: number;
  staleSources: number;
};

export type DailyPlanTask = {
  order: number;
  type: 'buy' | 'sell' | 'review' | 'operator' | 'sync';
  title: string;
  reason: string;
};

@Injectable()
export class DailyPlanService {
  generate(input: DailyPlanInput): DailyPlanTask[] {
    const tasks: DailyPlanTask[] = [];
    let order = 1;

    if (input.staleSources > 0) {
      tasks.push({
        order: order++,
        type: 'sync',
        title: 'Refresh stale market sources',
        reason: 'Source freshness affects all opportunity quality',
      });
    }

    if (input.unresolvedCount > 0) {
      tasks.push({
        order: order++,
        type: 'operator',
        title: 'Resolve unresolved matches',
        reason: 'Dirty listings block market intelligence quality',
      });
    }

    if (input.buyCount > 0) {
      tasks.push({
        order: order++,
        type: 'buy',
        title: 'Execute top buy opportunities',
        reason: 'Best spread and ROI should be acted on early',
      });
    }

    if (input.sellCount > 0) {
      tasks.push({
        order: order++,
        type: 'sell',
        title: 'List or reprice top sell candidates',
        reason: 'Unlock capital from strongest inventory exits',
      });
    }

    if (input.reviewCount > 0) {
      tasks.push({
        order: order++,
        type: 'review',
        title: 'Clear review queue',
        reason: 'Manual review is blocking execution throughput',
      });
    }

    if (tasks.length === 0) {
      tasks.push({
        order: 1,
        type: 'sync',
        title: 'Maintain dashboard and monitor new opportunities',
        reason: 'No critical execution queue detected today',
      });
    }

    return tasks;
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\flip-strategy.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { calculateRoiPercent, toMoney } from '../../common/money.utils';

export type FlipStrategyInput = {
  itemType: string;
  buyPrice: number;
  targetSellPrice: number;
  medianPrice: number;
  soldCount: number;
  volatility: number;
  confidenceScore: number;
};

export type FlipStrategyResult = {
  strategy: 'quick_flip' | 'hold_flip' | 'part_out' | 'avoid';
  score: number;
  reasonPrimary: string;
  reasonSecondary: string;
};

@Injectable()
export class FlipStrategyService {
  decide(input: FlipStrategyInput): FlipStrategyResult {
    const spread = toMoney(input.targetSellPrice - input.buyPrice);

    const roi = calculateRoiPercent({
      profit: spread,
      cost: input.buyPrice,
    });

    if (
      input.itemType === 'minifigure' &&
      input.soldCount >= 8 &&
      roi >= 18 &&
      input.volatility <= 0.3
    ) {
      return {
        strategy: 'quick_flip',
        score: 88,
        reasonPrimary: 'Fast minifigure turnover is likely',
        reasonSecondary: 'Strong sold count and controlled volatility',
      };
    }

    if (
      input.itemType === 'set' &&
      input.confidenceScore >= 0.6 &&
      input.medianPrice > input.buyPrice &&
      roi >= 22
    ) {
      return {
        strategy: 'hold_flip',
        score: 81,
        reasonPrimary: 'Set looks better as a hold flip',
        reasonSecondary: 'Price gap and confidence support waiting',
      };
    }

    if (
      input.itemType === 'set' &&
      input.soldCount >= 4 &&
      input.volatility >= 0.35 &&
      roi >= 25
    ) {
      return {
        strategy: 'part_out',
        score: 76,
        reasonPrimary: 'Set may be stronger as part-out candidate',
        reasonSecondary: 'Volatility suggests uneven component value',
      };
    }

    return {
      strategy: 'avoid',
      score: 40,
      reasonPrimary: 'Flip profile is not strong enough',
      reasonSecondary: 'Weak spread, weak velocity or weak confidence',
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\hold-time-estimator.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';

export type HoldTimeInput = {
  soldPerMonth: number;
  stockCount: number;
};

export type HoldTimeResult = {
  estimatedDays: number;
  liquidityTier: 'fast' | 'medium' | 'slow';
};

@Injectable()
export class HoldTimeEstimatorService {
  estimate(input: HoldTimeInput): HoldTimeResult {
    if (input.soldPerMonth <= 0) {
      return {
        estimatedDays: 999,
        liquidityTier: 'slow',
      };
    }

    const monthsToSell = input.stockCount / input.soldPerMonth;
    const days = monthsToSell * 30;

    let tier: HoldTimeResult['liquidityTier'] = 'medium';

    if (days <= 14) {
      tier = 'fast';
    } else if (days >= 45) {
      tier = 'slow';
    }

    return {
      estimatedDays: Math.round(days),
      liquidityTier: tier,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\liquidity-rank.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';

export type LiquidityRankInput = {
  soldCount: number;
  volatility: number;
  confidence: number;
};

export type LiquidityRankResult = {
  score: number;
  tier: 'A' | 'B' | 'C' | 'D';
};

@Injectable()
export class LiquidityRankService {
  rank(input: LiquidityRankInput): LiquidityRankResult {
    let score =
      input.soldCount * 5 +
      (1 - input.volatility) * 40 +
      input.confidence * 30;

    score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));

    let tier: LiquidityRankResult['tier'] = 'D';

    if (score >= 80) {
      tier = 'A';
    } else if (score >= 60) {
      tier = 'B';
    } else if (score >= 40) {
      tier = 'C';
    }

    return {
      score: Number(score.toFixed(2)),
      tier,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\minifigure-arbitrage.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  calculateRoiPercent,
  toMoney,
} from '../../common/money.utils';

export type MinifigureArbitrageInput = {
  buyPrice: number;
  targetSellPrice: number;
  soldCount: number;
  volatility: number;
  confidenceScore: number;
};

export type MinifigureArbitrageResult = {
  isArbitrage: boolean;
  score: number;
  netSpread: number;
  roiPercent: number;
  reason: string;
};

@Injectable()
export class MinifigureArbitrageService {
  evaluate(input: MinifigureArbitrageInput): MinifigureArbitrageResult {
    const netSpread = toMoney(input.targetSellPrice - input.buyPrice);

    const roiPercent = calculateRoiPercent({
      profit: netSpread,
      cost: input.buyPrice,
    });

    if (
      netSpread >= 120 &&
      roiPercent >= 20 &&
      input.soldCount >= 6 &&
      input.volatility <= 0.35 &&
      input.confidenceScore >= 0.55
    ) {
      return {
        isArbitrage: true,
        score: 90,
        netSpread,
        roiPercent,
        reason: 'Strong minifigure arbitrage candidate',
      };
    }

    if (netSpread >= 70 && roiPercent >= 12 && input.soldCount >= 3) {
      return {
        isArbitrage: true,
        score: 72,
        netSpread,
        roiPercent,
        reason: 'Moderate minifigure arbitrage candidate',
      };
    }

    return {
      isArbitrage: false,
      score: 35,
      netSpread,
      roiPercent,
      reason: 'Weak minifigure arbitrage profile',
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\minifigure-pricing.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { toMoney } from '../../common/money.utils';

export type MinifigurePricingInput = {
  medianPrice: number;
  lowestPrice: number;
  soldCount: number;
  volatility: number;
};

export type MinifigurePricingResult = {
  quickSellPrice: number;
  marketPrice: number;
  premiumPrice: number;
};

@Injectable()
export class MinifigurePricingService {
  suggest(input: MinifigurePricingInput): MinifigurePricingResult {
    let quickSell = input.lowestPrice;
    let market = input.medianPrice;
    let premium = input.medianPrice * 1.12;

    if (input.soldCount >= 8) {
      quickSell = input.lowestPrice + 10;
      market = input.medianPrice;
      premium = input.medianPrice * 1.18;
    }

    if (input.volatility >= 0.4) {
      quickSell *= 0.95;
      premium *= 0.95;
    }

    return {
      quickSellPrice: toMoney(quickSell),
      marketPrice: toMoney(market),
      premiumPrice: toMoney(premium),
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\part-out-estimator.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  addMoney,
  calculateRoiPercent,
  toMoney,
} from '../../common/money.utils';

export type PartOutComponent = {
  name: string;
  estimatedPrice: number;
  liquidity: number;
};

export type PartOutInput = {
  itemId: string;
  components: PartOutComponent[];
  buyPrice: number;
};

export type PartOutResult = {
  totalEstimatedValue: number;
  netAfterFees: number;
  roiPercent: number;
  liquidityScore: number;
  recommendation: 'strong_part_out' | 'moderate_part_out' | 'avoid';
};

@Injectable()
export class PartOutEstimatorService {
  estimate(input: PartOutInput): PartOutResult {
    const totalEstimatedValue = addMoney(
      ...input.components.map((component) => component.estimatedPrice),
    );

    const avgLiquidity =
      input.components.length > 0
        ? input.components.reduce(
            (sum, component) => sum + component.liquidity,
            0,
          ) / input.components.length
        : 0;

    const feeFactor = 0.13;
    const netAfterFees = toMoney(totalEstimatedValue * (1 - feeFactor));

    const profit = toMoney(netAfterFees - input.buyPrice);

    const roiPercent = calculateRoiPercent({
      profit,
      cost: input.buyPrice,
    });

    let recommendation: PartOutResult['recommendation'] = 'avoid';

    if (roiPercent >= 35 && avgLiquidity >= 0.6) {
      recommendation = 'strong_part_out';
    } else if (roiPercent >= 18 && avgLiquidity >= 0.4) {
      recommendation = 'moderate_part_out';
    }

    return {
      totalEstimatedValue,
      netAfterFees,
      roiPercent,
      liquidityScore: Number(avgLiquidity.toFixed(2)),
      recommendation,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\profit-tracker.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  addMoney,
  calculateProfit,
  calculateRoiPercent,
} from '../../common/money.utils';

export type ProfitTrackerTrade = {
  buyCost: number;
  sellRevenue: number;
  fees: number;
  shipping: number;
  packaging: number;
};

export type ProfitTrackerResult = {
  totalBuyCost: number;
  totalRevenue: number;
  totalFees: number;
  totalOverhead: number;
  netProfit: number;
  roiPercent: number;
};

@Injectable()
export class ProfitTrackerService {
  summarize(trades: ProfitTrackerTrade[]): ProfitTrackerResult {
    const totalBuyCost = addMoney(...trades.map((trade) => trade.buyCost));
    const totalRevenue = addMoney(...trades.map((trade) => trade.sellRevenue));
    const totalFees = addMoney(...trades.map((trade) => trade.fees));
    const totalOverhead = addMoney(
      ...trades.map((trade) => trade.shipping + trade.packaging),
    );

    const netProfit = calculateProfit({
      revenue: totalRevenue,
      cost: addMoney(totalBuyCost, totalFees, totalOverhead),
    });

    const roiPercent = calculateRoiPercent({
      profit: netProfit,
      cost: totalBuyCost,
    });

    return {
      totalBuyCost,
      totalRevenue,
      totalFees,
      totalOverhead,
      netProfit,
      roiPercent,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\risk-management.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { toMoney } from '../../common/money.utils';

export type RiskManagementInput = {
  buyPrice: number;
  expectedNetProfit: number;
  roiPercent: number;
  liquidityScore: number;
  volatility: number;
  confidenceScore: number;
};

export type RiskManagementResult = {
  riskScore: number;
  riskTier: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';
  maxPositionSize: number;
  stopBuyPrice: number;
  reason: string;
};

@Injectable()
export class RiskManagementService {
  evaluate(input: RiskManagementInput): RiskManagementResult {
    let riskScore = 50;

    riskScore -= input.roiPercent * 0.35;
    riskScore -= input.expectedNetProfit / 20;
    riskScore -= input.liquidityScore * 25;
    riskScore += input.volatility * 45;
    riskScore -= input.confidenceScore * 18;

    riskScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, riskScore));

    let riskTier: RiskManagementResult['riskTier'] = 'medium';
    let maxPositionSize = 0.2;
    let reason = 'Balanced exposure is recommended';

    if (riskScore < 35) {
      riskTier = 'low';
      maxPositionSize = 0.35;
      reason = 'Low risk profile allows larger position sizing';
    } else if (riskScore > 65) {
      riskTier = 'high';
      maxPositionSize = 0.12;
      reason = 'High risk profile requires smaller position sizing';
    }

    return {
      riskScore: Number(riskScore.toFixed(2)),
      riskTier,
      maxPositionSize,
      stopBuyPrice: toMoney(input.buyPrice * (1 + input.volatility * 0.3)),
      reason,
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\strategy\smart-pricing.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { toMoney } from '../../common/money.utils';

export type SmartPricingInput = {
  costBasis: number;
  lowestMarketPrice: number;
  medianMarketPrice: number;
  soldCount: number;
  volatility: number;
  strategy: 'quick_flip' | 'hold_flip' | 'part_out' | 'avoid';
};

export type SmartPricingResult = {
  suggestedPrice: number;
  floorPrice: number;
  stretchPrice: number;
  reason: string;
};

@Injectable()
export class SmartPricingService {
  suggest(input: SmartPricingInput): SmartPricingResult {
    let suggestedPrice = input.medianMarketPrice;

    if (input.strategy === 'quick_flip') {
      suggestedPrice = Math.min(
        input.medianMarketPrice,
        input.lowestMarketPrice + 20,
      );
    } else if (input.strategy === 'hold_flip') {
      suggestedPrice = Math.max(
        input.medianMarketPrice,
        input.lowestMarketPrice + 80,
      );
    } else if (input.strategy === 'part_out') {
      suggestedPrice = input.medianMarketPrice * 1.06;
    }

    if (input.soldCount <= 2) {
      suggestedPrice *= 0.96;
    }

    if (input.volatility >= 0.4) {
      suggestedPrice *= 0.94;
    }

    const floorPrice = Math.max(input.costBasis * 1.08, input.lowestMarketPrice);
    const stretchPrice = Math.max(suggestedPrice * 1.08, input.medianMarketPrice);

    return {
      suggestedPrice: toMoney(suggestedPrice),
      floorPrice: toMoney(floorPrice),
      stretchPrice: toMoney(stretchPrice),
      reason: 'Smart price based on market position and flip strategy',
    };
  }
}

  CapitalAllocationCandidate,

} from './capital-allocation.service';

  ProfitTrackerService,
  ProfitTrackerTrade,
} from './profit-tracker.service';

  RiskManagementInput,
  RiskManagementService,
} from './risk-management.service';

@Controller('strategy')
export class StrategyController {
  constructor(
    private readonly capitalAllocationService: CapitalAllocationService,
    private readonly riskManagementService: RiskManagementService,
    private readonly profitTrackerService: ProfitTrackerService,
  ) {}

  @Post('allocate')
  allocate(
    @Body()
    body: {
      capital: number;
      candidates: CapitalAllocationCandidate[];
    },
  ) {
    return this.capitalAllocationService.allocate(
      body.capital,
      body.candidates ?? [],
    );
  }

  @Post('risk')
  risk(@Body() body: RiskManagementInput) {
    return this.riskManagementService.evaluate(body);
  }

  @Post('profit')
  profit(
    @Body()
    body: {
      trades: ProfitTrackerTrade[];
    },
  ) {
    return this.profitTrackerService.summarize(body.trades ?? []);
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\suggestions\suggestions.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { toMoney } from '../../common/money.utils';
import { NotificationsService } from '../notifications/notifications.service';
import { PricingService } from '../pricing/pricing.service';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';

@Injectable()
export class SuggestionsService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly pricing: PricingService,
    private readonly notifications: NotificationsService,
  ) {}

  async getBuySuggestions(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const watchlist = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.findMany({
      include: {
        item: true,
      },
    });

    const result = watchlist.map((item) => {
      const buyPrice = toMoney(item.maxBuyPrice);
      const sellPrice = toMoney(item.targetSellPrice ?? 0);

      const roi = this.pricing.calculateROI({
        buyPrice,
        sellPrice,
      });

      const action =
        roi > 50
          ? 'BUY NOW'
          : roi > 30
            ? 'GOOD DEAL'
            : 'SKIP';

      return {
        id: item.id,
        itemId: item.itemId,
        title: item.titleSnapshot,
        setNumber: item.item?.setNumber ?? null,
        buyPrice,
        sellPrice,
        roi,
        action,
      };
    });

    const best = result.find((item) => item.action === 'BUY NOW');

    if (best) {
      await this.notifications.createDealNotification({
        itemTitle: best.title,
        roi: best.roi,
        action: best.action,
      });
    }

    return result;
  }

  async getSellSuggestions(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    const inventory = await this.prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
      include: {
        item: true,
      },
    });

    return inventory.map((item) => {
      const buyPrice = toMoney(item.totalCost);
      const sellPrice = toMoney(item.expectedSalePriceManual ?? 0);

      const roi = this.pricing.calculateROI({
        buyPrice,
        sellPrice,
      });

      return {
        id: item.id,
        itemId: item.itemId,
        title: item.titleSnapshot,
        setNumber: item.item?.setNumber ?? null,
        buyPrice,
        sellPrice,
        roi,
        action:
          roi > 60
            ? 'SELL NOW'
            : roi > 30
              ? 'LIST'
              : 'HOLD',
      };
    });
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\sync\sync-orchestrator.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { DecisionsService } from '../decisions/decisions.service';
import { MarketSyncService } from '../market/market-sync.service';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { SyncStateService } from './sync-state.service';

@Injectable()
export class SyncOrchestratorService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly marketSyncService: MarketSyncService,
    private readonly decisionsService: DecisionsService,
    private readonly syncStateService: SyncStateService,
  ) {}

  async refreshAll(): Promise<{
    totalItems: number;
    refreshedItems: number;
    recomputedInventoryDecisions: number;
    recomputedWatchlistDecisions: number;
  }> {
    const [items, inventoryItems, watchlistItems] = await Promise.all([
      this.prisma.item.findMany({
        select: {
          id: true,
        },
      }),
      this.prisma.inventoryItem.findMany({
        select: {
          id: true,
        },
      }),
      this.prisma.watchlistItem.findMany({
        select: {
          id: true,
        },
      }),
    ]);

    this.syncStateService.start('global_refresh', items.length);

    let refreshedItems = 0;
    let recomputedInventoryDecisions = 0;
    let recomputedWatchlistDecisions = 0;

    try {
      for (const item of items) {
        await this.marketSyncService.refreshItemSnapshot(item.id);
        refreshedItems += 1;
        this.syncStateService.progress(refreshedItems);
      }

      for (const inventoryItem of inventoryItems) {
        await this.decisionsService.recomputeInventoryDecision(inventoryItem.id);
        recomputedInventoryDecisions += 1;
      }

      for (const watchlistItem of watchlistItems) {
        await this.decisionsService.recomputeWatchlistDecision(watchlistItem.id);
        recomputedWatchlistDecisions += 1;
      }

      this.syncStateService.finish();

      return {
        totalItems: items.length,
        refreshedItems,
        recomputedInventoryDecisions,
        recomputedWatchlistDecisions,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      this.syncStateService.fail();
      throw error;
    }
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\sync\sync-state.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';

type SyncRunState = {
  isRunning: boolean;
  startedAt: Date | null;
  finishedAt: Date | null;
  lastMode: string | null;
  processedItems: number;
  totalItems: number;
};

@Injectable()
export class SyncStateService {
  private state: SyncRunState = {
    isRunning: false,
    startedAt: null,
    finishedAt: null,
    lastMode: null,
    processedItems: 0,
    totalItems: 0,
  };

  start(mode: string, totalItems: number): void {
    this.state = {
      isRunning: true,
      startedAt: new Date(),
      finishedAt: null,
      lastMode: mode,
      processedItems: 0,
      totalItems,
    };
  }

  progress(processedItems: number): void {
    this.state = {
      ...this.state,
      processedItems,
    };
  }

  finish(): void {
    this.state = {
      ...this.state,
      isRunning: false,
      finishedAt: new Date(),
      processedItems: this.state.totalItems,
    };
  }

  fail(): void {
    this.state = {
      ...this.state,
      isRunning: false,
      finishedAt: new Date(),
    };
  }

  getState(): SyncRunState {
    return this.state;
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\sync\sync.controller.ts =====

import { Controller, Get, Param, Post, UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { JwtAuthGuard } from '../auth/jwt-auth.guard';
import { Roles } from '../auth/roles.decorator';
import { RolesGuard } from '../auth/roles.guard';
import { SyncOrchestratorService } from './sync-orchestrator.service';
import { SyncStateService } from './sync-state.service';
import { SyncService } from './sync.service';

@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard)
@Roles('admin', 'operator')
@Controller('sync')
export class SyncController {
  constructor(
    private readonly syncService: SyncService,
    private readonly syncStateService: SyncStateService,
    private readonly syncOrchestratorService: SyncOrchestratorService,
  ) {}

  @Get('item/:itemId/status')
  async getItemSyncStatus(@Param('itemId') itemId: string): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.syncService.getItemSyncStatus(itemId);
  }

  @Get('dashboard/summary')
  async getDashboardSyncSummary(): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.syncService.getDashboardSyncSummary();
  }

  @Get('state')
  getSyncState(): unknown {
    return this.syncStateService.getState();
  }

  @Post('refresh-all')
  async refreshAll(): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.syncOrchestratorService.refreshAll();
  }
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\sync\sync.service.ts =====

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';

type SyncStatusResult = {
  itemId: string;
  latestSnapshotAt: Date | null;
  latestDecisionAt: Date | null;
  latestListingAt: Date | null;
  snapshotFreshnessLabel: string;
  needsRefresh: boolean;
};

@Injectable()
export class SyncService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  private freshnessLabel(date: Date | null): string {
    if (date == null) {
      return 'missing';
    }

    const diffHours = (Date.now() - date.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60);

    if (diffHours <= 6) return 'fresh';
    if (diffHours <= 24) return 'recent';
    if (diffHours <= 72) return 'aging';
    if (diffHours <= 168) return 'stale';

    return 'very stale';
  }

  private needsRefresh(date: Date | null): boolean {
    if (date == null) {
      return true;
    }

    const diffHours = (Date.now() - date.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    return diffHours > 24;
  }

  async logSyncError(params: {
    scope: string;
    sourceCode?: string;
    referenceId?: string;
    message: string;
    detailsJson?: object;
  }): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.prisma.syncErrorLog.create({
      data: {
        scope: params.scope,
        sourceCode: params.sourceCode,
        referenceId: params.referenceId,
        message: params.message,
        detailsJson: params.detailsJson ?? {},
      },
    });
  }

  async getItemSyncStatus(itemId: string): Promise<SyncStatusResult> {
    const [latestSnapshot, latestDecision, latestListing] = await Promise.all([
      this.prisma.marketSnapshot.findFirst({
        where: {
          itemId,
        },
        orderBy: {
          computedAt: 'desc',
        },
        select: {
          computedAt: true,
        },
      }),
      this.prisma.decisionSnapshot.findFirst({
        where: {
          itemId,
        },
        orderBy: {
          createdAt: 'desc',
        },
        select: {
          createdAt: true,
        },
      }),
      this.prisma.marketListing.findFirst({
        where: {
          itemId,
        },
        orderBy: {
          fetchedAt: 'desc',
        },
        select: {
          fetchedAt: true,
        },
      }),
    ]);

    return {
      itemId,
      latestSnapshotAt: latestSnapshot?.computedAt ?? null,
      latestDecisionAt: latestDecision?.createdAt ?? null,
      latestListingAt: latestListing?.fetchedAt ?? null,
      snapshotFreshnessLabel: this.freshnessLabel(
        latestSnapshot?.computedAt ?? null,
      ),
      needsRefresh: this.needsRefresh(latestSnapshot?.computedAt ?? null),
    };
  }

  async getDashboardSyncSummary(): Promise<{
    totalItems: number;
    fresh: number;
    recent: number;
    aging: number;
    stale: number;
    veryStale: number;
    missing: number;
  }> {
    const items = await this.prisma.item.findMany({
      select: {
        id: true,
      },
    });

    let fresh = 0;
    let recent = 0;
    let aging = 0;
    let stale = 0;
    let veryStale = 0;
    let missing = 0;

    await Promise.all(
      items.map(async (item) => {
        const latestSnapshot = await this.prisma.marketSnapshot.findFirst({
          where: {
            itemId: item.id,
          },
          orderBy: {
            computedAt: 'desc',
          },
          select: {
            computedAt: true,
          },
        });

        const label = this.freshnessLabel(latestSnapshot?.computedAt ?? null);

        if (label === 'fresh') fresh++;
        else if (label === 'recent') recent++;
        else if (label === 'aging') aging++;
        else if (label === 'stale') stale++;
        else if (label === 'very stale') veryStale++;
        else missing++;
      }),
    );

    return {
      totalItems: items.length,
      fresh,
      recent,
      aging,
      stale,
      veryStale,
      missing,
    };
  }
}

import { Body, Controller, Delete, Get, Patch, Post } from '@nestjs/common';
import { CreateWatchlistItemDto } from './dto/create-watchlist-item.dto';
import { WatchlistService } from './watchlist.service';

@Controller('watchlist')
export class WatchlistController {
  constructor(private readonly service: WatchlistService) {}

  @Get()
  async getWatchlist(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.service.list();
  }

  @Post()
  async createWatchlist(
    @Body() body: CreateWatchlistItemDto,
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.service.create(body);
  }

  @Patch()
  async updateWatchlist(
    @Body()
    body: {
      id: string;
      desiredBuyPrice?: number;
      maxBuyPrice?: number;
      targetSellPrice?: number | null;
      active?: boolean;
      titleSnapshot?: string;
      priority?: number;
      notes?: string | null;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.service.update(body.id, body);
  }

  @Delete()
  async deleteWatchlist(
    @Body()
    body: {
      id: string;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    return this.service.delete(body.id);
  }

  @Patch('bulk-activate')

    @Body()
    body: {

    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {

  }

  async bulkDeleteWatchlist(
    @Body()

    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\api\src\modules\watchlist\watchlist.service.ts =====

import {
  BadRequestException,
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { RealtimeGateway } from '../realtime/realtime.gateway';
import { CreateWatchlistItemDto } from './dto/create-watchlist-item.dto';

@Injectable()
export class WatchlistService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    private readonly realtime: RealtimeGateway,
  ) {}

  async list(): Promise<unknown[]> {
    return this.prisma.watchlistItem.findMany({
      orderBy: {
        createdAt: 'desc',
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
        assignedUser: true,
      },
    });
  }

  async create(dto: CreateWatchlistItemDto): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!dto.itemId) {
      throw new BadRequestException('itemId is required');
    }

    const created = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.create({
      data: {
        itemId: dto.itemId,
        titleSnapshot: dto.titleSnapshot,
        desiredBuyPrice: dto.desiredBuyPrice,
        maxBuyPrice: dto.maxBuyPrice,
        targetSellPrice: dto.targetSellPrice ?? null,
        active: dto.active ?? true,
        priority: dto.priority ?? 50,
        notes: dto.notes,
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
        assignedUser: true,
      },
    });

    this.realtime.emitWatchlistRefresh(created);
    this.realtime.emitDashboardRefresh('watchlist_created');
    this.realtime.emitOpportunityRefresh('watchlist');

    return created;
  }

  async update(
    id: string,
    data: {
      desiredBuyPrice?: number;
      maxBuyPrice?: number;
      targetSellPrice?: number | null;
      active?: boolean;
      titleSnapshot?: string;
      priority?: number;
      notes?: string | null;
    },
  ): Promise<unknown> {
    const existing = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id,
      },
    });

    if (!existing) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Watchlist item not found');
    }

    const updated = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.update({
      where: {
        id,
      },
      data: {
        desiredBuyPrice: data.desiredBuyPrice,
        maxBuyPrice: data.maxBuyPrice,
        targetSellPrice: data.targetSellPrice,
        active: data.active,
        titleSnapshot: data.titleSnapshot,
        priority: data.priority,
        notes: data.notes,
      },
      include: {
        item: true,
        assignedUser: true,
      },
    });

    this.realtime.emitWatchlistRefresh(updated);
    this.realtime.emitDashboardRefresh('watchlist_updated');
    this.realtime.emitOpportunityRefresh('watchlist');

    return updated;
  }

  async delete(id: string): Promise<unknown> {
    const existing = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.findUnique({
      where: {
        id,
      },
    });

    if (!existing) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Watchlist item not found');
    }

    const deleted = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.delete({
      where: {
        id,
      },
    });

    this.realtime.emitWatchlistRefresh(deleted);
    this.realtime.emitDashboardRefresh('watchlist_deleted');
    this.realtime.emitOpportunityRefresh('watchlist');

    return deleted;
  }

  async bulkActivate(ids: string[], active: boolean): Promise<unknown> {
    const result = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.updateMany({
      where: {
        id: {
          in: ids ?? [],
        },
      },
      data: {
        active,
      },
    });

    this.realtime.emitWatchlistRefresh({
      ids,
      active,
      count: result.count,
    });
    this.realtime.emitDashboardRefresh('watchlist_bulk_activate');
    this.realtime.emitOpportunityRefresh('watchlist');

    return result;
  }

  async bulkDelete(ids: string[]): Promise<unknown> {
    const result = await this.prisma.watchlistItem.deleteMany({
      where: {
        id: {
          in: ids ?? [],
        },
      },
    });

    this.realtime.emitWatchlistRefresh({
      ids,
      count: result.count,
    });
    this.realtime.emitDashboardRefresh('watchlist_bulk_delete');
    this.realtime.emitOpportunityRefresh('watchlist');

    return result;
  }
}

import {
  IsBoolean,
  IsInt,
  IsNumber,
  IsOptional,
  IsString,
  Max,
  Min,
} from 'class-validator';

export class CreateWatchlistItemDto {
  @IsString()
  itemId!: string;

  @IsString()
  titleSnapshot!: string;

  @IsNumber()
  desiredBuyPrice!: number;

  @IsNumber()
  maxBuyPrice!: number;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsNumber()
  targetSellPrice?: number;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsBoolean()
  active?: boolean;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsInt()
  @Min(0)
  @Max(100)
  priority?: number;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  notes?: string;
}

import 'dotenv/config';
import { prisma } from './prisma';
import { runBrickLinkSource } from './sources/bricklink/bricklink-source';
import { runOlxSource } from './sources/olx/olx-source';

async function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
  await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function runAll(): Promise<void> {
  await runOlxSource();
  await runBrickLinkSource();
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  while (true) {
    try {
      await runAll();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    }
    await sleep(1000 * 60 * 20);
  }
}

main().catch(async (error) => {
  console.error(error);
  await prisma.$disconnect();
  process.exit(1);
});

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\scrapers\src\common\currency-converter.ts =====

export type CurrencyCode = 'UAH' | 'USD' | 'EUR';

const ratesToUah: Record<CurrencyCode, number> = {
  UAH: 1,
  USD: 41.5,
  EUR: 45.2,
};

export function convertToUah(
  amount: number,
  currency: string | null | undefined,
): number {
  const normalized = (currency ?? 'UAH').toUpperCase() as CurrencyCode;
  const rate = ratesToUah[normalized] ?? 1;

  return Number((amount * rate).toFixed(2));
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\Vlad\lego_trading_manager\backend\scrapers\src\common\item-matcher.ts =====

import { prisma } from '../prisma';
import { extractSetNumber, normalizeTitle } from './title-normalizer';

export async function resolveItemIdFromTitle(
  rawTitle: string,
): Promise<string | null> {
  const setNumber = extractSetNumber(rawTitle);

  if (setNumber) {
    const exact = await prisma.item.findFirst({
      where: {
        setNumber,
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
      },
    });

    if (exact) {
      return exact.id;
    }
  }

  const normalized = normalizeTitle(rawTitle);

  const items = await prisma.item.findMany({
    select: {
      id: true,
      title: true,
      setNumber: true,
    },
    take: 500,
  });

  for (const item of items) {
    const normalizedItemTitle = normalizeTitle(item.title);

    if (
      normalized.includes(normalizedItemTitle) ||
      normalizedItemTitle.includes(normalized)
    ) {
      return item.id;
    }
  }

  return null;
}

}
